Q: Can China's AI models continue to close the performance gap at lower R&D costs?
Likely. We observe a narrowing gap in model intelligence between China's providers and global
leaders, yet R&D spending at China's AI labs remains considerably lower than those of US peers.

While distillation improves efficiency in model development, we believe China's model providers
adopt multiple approaches to reduce workloads (such as smaller parameters and sparse attention)
and improve GPU throughput, supported by targeted R&D and open-source ecosystems.
Q: Can China's models keep prices competitive for API services while  maintaining
reasonable gross margins?
Likely. We believe Chinese AI models' competitive API prices are not driven by high subsidies or a loss-

leading strategy, but by structural advantages in inference efficiency. We note key technological
innovations across: 1) model architecture such as mixture of experts (MoE), attention mechanisms,
KV cache and lower precision; 2) engineering optimisation, including P/D separation, batching and
scheduling; and 3) infrastructure, such as domestic chip adaptation. Despite prices at under 20% of
those of comparable US peers, API gross margins for China's AI models are around 20-40%, with

upside potential  if prices improve with model performance or additional gains in inference efficiency.
Q: How will China's AI models reshape the global model competitive landscape?
We view competition as a secondary concern in light of the rapidly expanding global AI TAM, which
we estimate could surpass US$10trn. As model supply is concentrated in the US and China, we
believe China's model providers should have meaningful opportunities for global expansion. As AI

adoption scales, we also expect demand to become increasingly tiered. While frontier models may
retain pricing premiums for complex, high-value tasks, cost-performance models could see broader
adoption in high-volume, ROI-sensitive workloads, creating global share-gain opportunities for
China's AI models in the longer term.
As AI adoption moves from experimentation to scaled deployment, we believe cost efficiency is likely
to become a key competitive factor alongside model intelligence. Based on our detailed technical

analysis, we believe China's AI model providers demonstrate structural and sustainable advantages in
training and inference efficiency, which make them well positioned to gain share in the large,
expanding global AI TAM, in our view. We highlight MiniMax, Zhipu, Alibaba and Tencent under our
coverage as major companies in this theme, together with private companies DeepSeek, Kimi,
ByteDance and StepFun.
1) China's frontier models have reached around 90% of the intelligence level of leading US models,

improving from around 60% in 2023, according to Artificial Analysis. 2) Total R&D costs of China's
listed AI model developers Zhipu/MiniMax amounted to US$0.5bn/US$0.3bn in 2025. This is around
one-tenth of Anthropic's R&D spending, and even lower relative to  OpenAI. 3) API pricing for China's
model  is significantly lower than those of US peers. Overall, we estimate API prices for China's
frontier models averages below 20% of comparable  US peers with similar  intelligence. 4) MiniMax's

M2.7 has gross margins of over 40%, broadly in line with Anthropic (roughly 40% in 2025, mainly
API and B2B-driven), and also comparable to  OpenAI's 40%/33% gross margin in 2024/25, which
benefits from a larger revenue contribution from consumer-facing, subscription-based services. Our
channel checks indicate API gross margins for China's major models are roughly in the 20-40%
range.
We believe investors do not fully appreciate or have strong conviction in Chinese AI labs' cost

leadership, and instead expect materially higher R&D investments and widening losses to support
model training and rising token demand. Investors seem also concerned that model offerings will
become commoditised over time, leading to intense price competition and potentially unsustainable
business models for China's AI model providers.

### Page 4

China Internet Sector 16 June 2026 ab 4
Figure 2: Glossary of key AI model concepts
Category Concept Explanation
Token The basic unit of text processed by a model. One token may correspond to a Chinese character or part of an
English word.
Parameters The internal weights of a model. More parameters usually indicate larger model capacity, but also higher
training and inference cost.
Context window The maximum amount of text / tokens a model can process in one request. A longer context window

supports longer documents and agentic tasks.
Long-horizon task A complex task that requires many steps, tool calls or extended context, such as AI coding, debugging or
project planning.
AI agent An AI system that can plan, use tools and take actions to complete tasks, rather than only responding in a
chatbot format.
Tool use / function calling The ability of a model to call external tools, APIs, databases or software functions to retrieve information or

execute actions.
MoE (Mixture-of-Experts) A model architecture where only a subset of parameters is activated for each token, reducing compute
requirements.
Sparse attention A design that allows the model to focus on the most relevant parts of the context rather than every token.
Training / inference precision The numerical format used in model computation, such as FP16, BF16 and FP8. Lower precision can reduce
compute and memory cost while maintaining model quality.

Quantization Reducing the numerical precision of model weights or computation to lower memory usage and inference
cost, often with limited impact on model quality.
Prefill The stage where the model reads and processes the input prompt before generating any output.
Decode The stage where the model generates output tokens one by one.
KV cache A stored representation of previously processed tokens that avoids recomputing the full context during
inference.

Prompt caching / cached input Previously processed input tokens that can be reused and charged at a lower cached-input price, instead of
being recomputed and charged as full input.
MFU (Model FLOPs Utilisation) A measure of how effectively a GPU’s theoretical compute capacity is utilised during model training or
inference.
Batching Grouping multiple requests together so GPUs can process them more efficiently.
Scheduling The system-level process of assigning requests to GPUs and managing task execution to improve utilisation

and latency.
Prefill-Decode separation Separating prefill and decode workloads onto different GPU resources to improve utilisation and reduce
latency.
TPS (tokens per second) The speed at which a model generates output tokens. Higher TPS means faster response generation.
TTFT (time to first token) The time between submitting a request and receiving the first output token. Lower TTFT improves perceived
latency.
GPU-hour One GPU running for one hour. It is a common unit for measuring training or inference compute cost.

Model router A system that selects the most suitable model for each task based on quality, cost, latency, reliability or
other routing rules.
AI gateway A unified access layer for multiple models / providers, often handling model routing, API management,
monitoring, security and cost control.
API pricing The price charged for model usage, usually based on tokens. Pricing is commonly split into uncached input
tokens, cached input tokens and output tokens.

Token plan A prepaid or subscription-style model usage package, often with a fixed monthly fee and token quota /
allowance.
MaaS (Model-as-a-Service) A business model where customers access models through APIs or cloud platforms and pay based on
usage.

system

Closing the model performance gap at lower R&D
costs
We see a narrowing performance gap between AI models from the US and
China. While US remains at the forefront globally in model intelligence, China's model
providers are iterating rapidly and are catching up on key capabilities in:
 Overall intelligence of text models : China's frontier models have reached
around 90% of the intelligence level of top US models, improving from about

60% in 2023, according to data from Artificial Analysis.
 AI coding: China's leading models such as Qwen3.7-Max and DeepSeek V4-Pro
are comparable with previous generations of overseas models  (for example,
Claude Opus 4.6), although China's models still lag the latest frontier models such
as Claude Fable 5  and GPT-5.5.
 Multimodality: We find it encouraging that China's AI models have
demonstrated their global competitiveness. For instance, four of the top five

video-generation models globally are from China's developers, according to
Artificial Analysis.

China's AI labs are narrowing the performance gap at notably lower R&D
spending than US peers. Total R&D costs of listed Chinese AI model developers Zhipu/
MiniMax were US$0.5bn/US$0.3bn in 2025. This represents only around one-tenth of
Anthropic's R&D spending, and even less relative to  OpenAI, even when considering
only  server costs (that is computing costs) for the two US leaders. In our view, this
suggests China’s  progress reflects a catch-up in  capability as well as significantly more

efficient R&D spending.

Distillation helps, but does not fully explain Chinese companies' efficient model

development.  We view model distillation as a teacher-student process, in which a
'student' model (often with fewer parameters) learns to mimic the outputs or behaviour
of a stronger 'teacher' model. Based on our channel checks with industry experts,
distillation can improve model capability with shorter training time and lower compute
costs, but its effectiveness is more visible in tasks with verifiable outcomes, repeatable
processes or easily imitable patterns. In contrast, distillation is less effective for

capabilities that are less verifiable, rely on complex multi-step processes or depend on
underlying architecture and system-level engineering ( Figure 7D is t illa t io n  h e lp s  in  v e r if ia b le  t a s k s  b u t  g e n e r a lly  h a s  lim it a t io n s  in  f r o n t ie r  c a p a b ilit ie s ). We believe  China's
progress in frontier models suggests capability gains beyond distillation alone. For
instance, Zhipu has demonstrated strong long-horizon reasoning, while MiniMax

models feature advanced multimodal capabilities.
Figure 7: Distillation helps in verifiable tasks but generally has limitations in frontier capabilities
Applicability Task type Explanation
Verifiable tasks
(e.g., maths, basic coding, fixed benchmark
questions, exam-style reasoning)
Answers are verifiable; teacher reasoning traces and outputs can be
converted into high-quality training data; student models can catch
up through large-scale samples.

Imitable output patterns
(e.g., instruction following, answer style,
formatting preference, scenario-specific
response templates)
Student models can learn the teacher's output distribution and user-
preferred response style, even without fully replicating the underlying
capability.
Long-horizon tasks
(e.g., agentic coding, multi-step workflow
execution, complex tool use)
These tasks depend on skill selection, tool calls, environment feedback
and error recovery, not just final answers; the full trajectory is harder

to observe and distil.
Native multimodality
(e.g., image, video, audio and text
understanding / generation)
Multimodal capability is not just converting visual / audio outputs into
text for imitation; frontier performance requires deeper fusion of text,
image, video and audio within the model architecture.
Architecture / system-dependent capabilities
(e.g., long context, low hallucination, inference
efficiency, KV-cache management, low-cost
deployment, data flywheel)

These capabilities rely more on model architecture, training data,
inference infrastructure, real-user feedback and engineering
organisation than on observing teacher outputs.

Demystifying  China AI labs' R&D efficiency
China's model labs follow a more focused R&D strategy
In terms of R&D spending allocation, we observe differing strategic focus
between the US and China.  For US frontier labs, we see a larger share of R&D
spending being used for experimentation and path exploration , reflecting a stronger
emphasis on pushing the upper limits of model intelligence, supported by greater

compute resources. These experiments may span pre-training (non-Transformer
architecture for example), data (such as data curation and synthetic-data recipes), and
post-training stages (including  reinforcement learning from human feedback [RLHF] and
reinforcement learning-based reasoning and alignment). Taking OpenAI as an example,
Epoch AI data suggests that around 90% of the R&D compute budget is spent on
experimentation, with only about 10% used for final model training.

In contrast, we believe China's model labs tend to follow a more focused R&D path ,
which could create meaningful cost-saving potential by reducing the trial-and-error
process. We think China’s different strategic choices versus the US are mainly driven by
two factors.
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

### Page 8

China Internet Sector 16 June 2026 ab 8
1. More constrained compute resources. China's labs appear to allocate a smaller
proportion of resources to broad experimentation, focusing instead on formal model
training, targeted capability improvements, and architecture and engineering
optimisation.
2. Open-source ecosystem helps spread experimentation costs across China's AI
labs. While individual labs in China tend to follow a more focused R&D path, the

broader open-source ecosystem allows technical learnings to diffuse across companies.
This means not every lab needs to independently repeat the same broad set of
experiments as closed-source US peers. Instead, once one lab validates an architecture,
training recipe or optimisation method, others can learn from it and build on top of it
(see examples below). In our view, this creates collective R&D leverage and lowers the
marginal cost of capability improvement for China's labs.

 Kimi K2 and GLM-5 have adopted MLA-like latent attention design following
DeepSeek’s earlier validation;
 Zhipu has adopted elements inspired by DeepSeek’s sparse attention mechanism;
 DeepSeek V4 has incorporated techniques related to Moonshot AI and Kimi’s
Muon optimiser;
 Qwen3 and Zhipu GLM-4.5 have implemented GRPO-style RL recipes following
DeepSeek.

Source: Company data, arXiv, UBS
Technological innovation to reduce training workload and improve

compute utilisation
Within R&D  spend, we analyse the largest cost component –  model training
compute costs. We estimate model training compute costs could account for 65-75%
of total R&D expenses for China's leading AI labs such as MiniMax and Zhipu. We think
model training compute costs can be interpreted as a function of the following key
factors, and we have  observed that China's AI labs are optimising several of these
factors.
 Total training workload, which is broadly determined by model size (namely the

number of parameters), and training token volume (generally related to dataset
size and attention mechanism);
 Effective GPU capacity, which depends on the GPU's peak performance and
actual utilisation during training, commonly measured by Model FLOPs Utilisation
(MFU). The model training workload is  divided by effective computing capacity per
GPU hour to derive the total GPU hours required.
 Cost per GPU hour , which is affected by utility costs and hardware efficiency

(such as  GPU).  Model training compute costs can be estimated by multiplying total
GPU hours required by the cost per GPU hour.
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

Source: Epoch AI,  arXiv, Company data, UBS
1. Lower training computational  workload from smaller parameters

and sparse attention
Smaller parameters help reduce model training workload but may also partly
explain  the performance gap. China's frontier models remain smaller than their US
counterparts on parameter scale. Total parameters for China's leading models are still
below 2trn (for example DeepSeek V4 at 1.6trn and Kimi K2.6 at around 1trn). For US
models, official data is limited due to their closed-source architectures, but academic
papers estimate leading models such as Claude Opus 4.6/ChatGPT 5.5 have scaled to

around 10trn/5trn parameters. That said, while smaller model size reduces training
workload for China's labs, it may also partly contribute to the capability gap versus US
frontier models, in our view.
Reduced training volume (in terms of effective token-interaction workload)
through sparse-attention architectures.  In Transformer models, attention
determines how each token incorporates information from other tokens in the
sequence. Full attention requires every token to interact with every other token, making

compute scale quadratically with context length. Sparse attention reduces this burden
by limiting attention to a subset of relevant tokens, thereby lowering effective token-to-
token interactions in long-context training and inference. Given more constrained
compute-resource availability, our channel checks of industry experts indicate China's AI
labs are putting more effort into sparse-attention designs to reduce compute
consumption. In comparison, US peers may place greater emphasis on model quality

(such as multi-step reasoning), where full attention remains a more robust but compute-
intensive design choice. For instance, DeepSeek V3.2 uses DeepSeek Sparse Attention
(DSA) to reduce the workload, while DeepSeek V4 adds context compression before the
sparse design based on V3.2 to improve long-context efficiency.
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

2. Increasing GPU throughput by adopting lower compute precision;

multiple approaches to improve MFU
As discussed in our training cost framework, in addition to reducing total compute
workload on the numerator side, AI labs can also lower total training costs by enlarging
the denominator, that is by increasing the effective computing capacity per GPU hour.
We see China's labs placing greater emphasis on lower compute precision.
Under the same GPU, lower precision can significantly increase throughput, as fewer
bits are used per value, enabling more calculations per unit time while reducing memory

usage and data movement. Taking the Nvidia B200 as an example, moving from BF16/
FP16 to FP8 can roughly double theoretical GPU throughput. While large-scale model
training has historically relied on BF16/FP16 mixed precision, we see China's AI labs
pushing the frontier of lower-precision training. Examples include DeepSeek-V3's FP8
mixed-precision training framework and DeepSeek V4's FP4 quantisation-aware training
for select model components. Meanwhile, Baidu's ERNIE 4.5 and 5.0 adopt FP8 mixed-

precision training, while Alibaba's Qwen3.5 introduces a native FP8 pipeline and FP8
end-to-end training techniques.
Multiple approaches to improve MFU.   China's AI labs are establishing dedicated AI
infrastructure teams to optimise compute resource utilisation. For instance, with its end-
to-end infrastructure team, MiniMax has achieved more than 75% MFU, a key indicator
of computational efficiency for model inference activities, higher than the industry

average of 40-50%. Specifically, MiniMax has implemented:
 Unified training and inference computing , with an intelligent scheduling
system that dynamically allocates computing resources between training and
inference tasks, maximising cluster utilisation without compromising
performance.
 Cross-cluster load balancing , which enables flexible resource distribution,
lowers latency and enhances reliability and disaster recovery.
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

3. Lower unit GPU-hour costs supported by utility efficiency
We note that China appears to have a relative advantage in GPU rental prices
versus the US for comparable GPUs. Taking Nvidia H100 rental prices as an example,
the average US price is about US$1.99-3.99/GPU-hour, while it is about 40% lower for
China, at about US$1.3-2.1/GPU-hour (Rmb9-15/GPU-hour). We believe lower utility

costs (typically a low-to-mid-teen percentage of data-centre total cost of ownership, or
TCO, according to SemiAnalysis), especially for electricity, could partly explain this gap,
although GPU rental prices are also affected by hardware amortisation, utilisation,
contract terms and providers' margins. In the US, we estimate the average electricity
price in key datacentre states such as Illinois and Georgia is about 7.9 cents/kWh, while
comparable regions in China average around 4.4 cents/kWh, or roughly 44% lower.

World-leading inference efficiency to support

Lower model pricing, but comparable gross margins. China's model API prices are
significantly lower than those of US peers. Overall, we estimate the average API prices
for China's frontier models is below 20% of comparable US models  with similar
intelligence. The pricing gap is more pronounced for some models: DeepSeek V4 Pro

and MiniMax M2.7 are priced at less than 5% of Anthropic’s Opus series, despite only a
modest capability gap.
However, the gap in gross margins appears much narrower than the pricing level.
MiniMax M2.7 has a gross margin of over 40%, broadly in line with Anthropic (about
40% in 2025, mainly API and B2B-driven), also comparable to  OpenAI's 40%/33%
gross margin in 2024/2025, which has a larger revenue contribution from consumer-
facing, subscription-based services. Based on our checks, API gross margins for China's

major models are roughly in the 20-40% range.

Similar to the high efficiency in model training, we see multiple inference-side
levers for China's model providers to optimise costs, supporting lower prices while
protecting margins. Specifically, we break down Chinese models’ inference efficiency

into three layers:
 Model architecture optimisation: Beyond smaller model sizes, China's labs are
adopting more sparse MoE architectures to reduce active compute. KV-cache
compression, built on sparse-attention designs, can further lower memory-related
costs, which we see as the largest inference cost component, while lower-
precision computation is also more widely used at the inference stage;
 Serving and engineering optimisation : Inference workloads are more

fragmented, real-time and heterogeneous than training, creating more room for
serving-layer optimisation such as P/D separation, batching and dynamic
scheduling, which can improve GPU utilisation and serving throughput;
 Resource and hardware optimisation : Similar to training, inference benefits
from China's energy-cost advantage, while domestic compute adoption appears
to be moving faster at the inference stage given workloads are more modular and

easier to optimise for specific models and use cases, in our view.

1. Model-side optimisation: Lower compute and memory usage
Under constrained compute resources, China's model labs continue to innovate in
model architecture to lower inference costs. Compared with training, inference provides

additional optimisation levers, allowing labs to reduce overall model scale, lower active
compute (through Mixture of Experts, or MoE), adopt sparse attention and compress
memory usage (namely KV-cache compression).
 Mixture of Experts reduces active parameters. China's frontier models tend to
have smaller total parameter scales than US counterparts, which lowers their
baseline compute requirements. At the inference stage, MoE provides an
additional layer of sparsity by routing each token to only a subset of expert

networks, rather than activating the full model for every token. While we
acknowledge that MoE is not unique to China and was initially developed by
overseas labs, China's model developers appear more proactive in deploying it at
scale, improving model sparsity (by lowering the active-parameter ratio) while still
enhancing model performance. Given limited visibility into closed-source overseas
models, we note that earlier overseas MoE models such as GPT-4, Grok-1 and

Llama 4 Scout typically activated about 15-30% of total parameters, while China's
leading MoE models usually activate only about 3-10%. We view DeepSeek as a
notable example: its MoE active-parameter ratio declined from about 5% in
DeepSeek V3.2 to about 3% in V4 Pro, while more efficient parameter activation
still supported further improvements in model intelligence (AA Intelligence Index
rising from 42 to 52).
 Sparse attention reduces long-context compute.  Similar to the training

phase, attention design is another inference-efficiency lever. Moving from full
attention to sparse attention can reduce the number of token interactions,
especially in long-context workloads such as AI coding, agents and long-
document tasks, lowering effective compute required per request. At the
inference stage, the sparse attention architecture can be combined with KV-cache
compression, further lowering compute workloads.
 While memory-related costs dominate inference costs, KV-cache

compression can significantly reduce them.  Based on an academic  paper,
cached input is the largest cost component in inference, accounting for about
70% of total costs in multi-round agent tasks, while the remaining costs come
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

### Page 15

China Internet Sector 16 June 2026 ab 15
from uncached input and  output. KV-cache compression can therefore materially
reduce total inference costs. Notably, the decline in cached-input prices was the
largest cost-saving driver for DeepSeek V4 pricing (V4 Pro's blended cost is about
10% lower than V3 despite significantly better performance, offsetting higher
input and output pricing). We believe this likely reflects DeepSeek's KV-cache
innovations (Heavily Compressed Attention [HCA] and Compressed Sparse

Attention [CSA]), with its technical report showing V4 requires only about 10% of
V3.2's KV cache at the same context length (1m tokens). Other AI labs in China
have adopted similar approaches (for example, Kimi's KDA). That said, overseas
vendors also explore KV-cache optimisation amid rising memory costs and long-
context serving pressure, such as Google's TurboQuant, although China's leading
labs appear more aggressive in embedding KV reduction into their model

architecture.
 Lower precision is easier to adopt in inference (i.e., quantisation) than in
training. While low-precision training requires careful control of model stability
and effective learning, inference generally has higher tolerance for lower-precision
computation. This can improve throughput and reduce memory usage with less
impact on model quality, adding another cost-saving lever at the inference stage.
We observe wider adoption of low inference precision across China's AI labs,

including both emerging AI labs (such as DeepSeek, Zhipu and Moonshot AI) as
well internet incumbents (such as Baidu, Alibaba and Tencent).

2. Serving and engineering optimisation: Higher throughput from
better workload orchestration
Beyond model architecture, AI developers can further reduce inference costs by
optimising the AI infrastructure layer (namely compute clusters), improving chip
utilisation and serving throughput. Compared with training, where optimisation is

mainly focused on keeping large-scale clusters stable and improving MFU, inference
serving has more heterogeneous workloads and therefore more room for workload
separation, batching and real-time scheduling.
 P/D separation improves resource matching.  We view P/D separation as an
inference-specific lever. While training workloads are relatively more uniform, LLM
inference consists of two distinct phases: prefill (processing the input prompt and

building KV cache) and decode (generating output tokens). These two phases
have different resource profiles: prefill is more compute-heavy, while decode is
more memory- and bandwidth-heavy, as well as latency-sensitive. Prefill and
decode disaggregation separates the two workloads into different GPU pools,
allowing each phase to use more suitable resources and reducing interference
between long prompts and token generation.
 Batching improves GPU utilisation.  We view batching as more important in

inference than training. Training naturally processes large batches of data, while
inference demand arrives in fragmented, real-time user requests with different
prompt lengths and output lengths. Batching combines multiple inference
requests so GPUs process more tokens together rather than serving each request
independently. Continuous and in-flight batching further improves throughput by
admitting new requests as soon as finished sequences leave the batch, rather than

waiting for the slowest request to finish. This helps raise GPU utilisation and
reduces cost per token.
 Scheduling reduces idle time and load imbalance.  Similar to the training
phase, inference systems need to keep expensive GPUs working rather than
waiting. Better scheduling (e.g., unified training and inference compute, cross-
load balancing, routing between different model sizes and clusters) can reduce idle
time, smooth traffic spikes, and improve overall cluster utilisation.

This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

3. Resource and hardware costs: Accelerating compute power
localisation in inference stage
Energy cost advantage applies to training and inference. Similar to training, China
may benefit from lower electricity costs, resulting in lower GPU-hour rental prices for
comparable AI chips and providing a cost buffer for inference.
We see faster progress of domestic chip adaptation in inference than training.

Frontier training still requires highly stable large-scale clusters, mature software stacks,
high memory bandwidth and strong interconnect, where domestic alternatives still need
time to catch up, in our view. In contrast, inference workloads can be more modular and
easier to optimise for specific models and use cases, which we believe could make
domestic-chip deployment more feasible in the near term. That said, we also
acknowledge actual cost advantages still depend on utilisation, serving stability, model

compatibility and workload mix.
Figure 29: Summary of China's AI models adopting domestic chips

Looking ahead, we expect China's structural inference-cost advantages to
continue over the medium to longer term. We base this view on the following
factors:
1. The largest cost savings appear to come from model-layer innovation (such
as MoE sparsity, sparse attention, KV-cache compression and low precision), which
US frontier labs are unlikely to rapidly replicate by changing their underlying model
architectures purely to compete on cost, in our view. This is because pushing the

upper limits of model intelligence remains their strategic priority, while greater
compute resources may also lower their incentive to optimise for efficiency.
2. Resource and hardware advantages provide additional support.  China's
lower energy costs relative to the US remains an additional cost buffer. On
hardware, we also see China's AI labs and domestic chip suppliers working
together to gradually narrow the performance and efficiency gap with US chips.

For instance, DeepSeek noted that V4-Pro API prices could fall significantly after
Huawei's Ascend 950 SuperPod enters mass production in H226, highlighting the
This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

### Page 18

China Internet Sector 16 June 2026 ab 18
cost-saving potential of domestic-chip deployment. StepFun also launched Step 3
with domestic-chip deployment in mind and formed a model-chip ecosystem
alliance with nearly 10 domestic chip and infrastructure vendors to improve
adaptation and inference efficiency.
3. That said, we recognise engineering as a potential area where US labs
could narrow the gap.  Improved infrastructure orchestration (such as
scheduling, batching and serving-stack optimisation) could reduce inference costs

without requiring fundamental changes to model architecture.
Looking ahead, we expect continued optimisation across these three layers, i.e. model
architecture, serving efficiency and resource / hardware cost, to further reduce inference
cost per token. Meanwhile, mounting token demand and constraint in compute may
keep API prices resilient in the near term. In our view, this could create room for longer-
term gross margin expansion of China's model API services.

This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

### Page 19

China Internet Sector 16 June 2026 ab 19
>US$10trn long-term AI opportunity globally, with model
supply dominated by the US and China
In light of rapidly evolving AI and LLM development, we see a vast opportunity
in the global AI market , with long-term TAM potentially exceeding US$10trn. This
magnitude has been widely cited by industry leaders, including Nvidia's CEO Jensen
Huang and Alibaba's Chairman Joe Tsai. We think the underlying logic is similar: global

GDP is already above US$110trn; labour income, or services value added, accounts for
roughly 50-60% of GDP; and if AI augments or automates around 20% of the human-
labour and cognitive-work base, the addressable opportunity could surpass  US$10trn.
Beyond the US and China, we believe the monetisation potential in the rest of
world (RoW) should not be overlooked. Admittedly, AI adoption rates across
countries and regions are likely to be disproportionate, and the US and China may see

higher AI adoption, given their concentration of frontier AI labs and ecosystem
resources. Even if we assume a higher AI penetration rate of 30% for the US and only
15% for the RoW  reflecting the more limited access to  frontier models and AI
infrastructure, our calculation suggests the RoW's potential market size could still reach
US$4.7trn, comparable to the US market at US$4.9trn. That said, we recognise these
markets are more fragmented, and AI adoption in some countries may be constrained by

IT infrastructure development.

We also note that global AI model supply is increasingly dominated by the US
and China. In terms of quantity, from 2019 to 2025, the US and China trained 223 and
192 cumulative large-scale AI systems, respectively, far ahead of other regions and

nearly 10x more than France, the third-largest country. in terms of model capability, only
one of the top 20 models in the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index comes from France
(namely Mistral), while the rest are from US or Chinese companies. In our view,  this
suggests Chinese model companies have significant potential for global
expansion, even when focusing solely on markets  outside the US.

China Internet Sector 16 June 2026 ab 21
Framing potential upside in China AI's global share
To gauge the future market structure and potential upside in the global market share for
China's AI model companies, we use other 'China-going-global' industries as analogies
under three scenarios:
In a cost-led scenario, we believe China's model companies could gain
meaningful global share, underpinned by their structural cost advantages.  If
China's models eventually converge with global leaders in capability and model usage

becomes largely commoditised (i.e., users see limited difference when calling different
models), we believe the closest analogy could be solar photovoltaic (PV). The solar PV
market is largely driven by scale, cost and supply-chain competition, where China has
built a dominant global position, with an 80%-plus global share and up to about 95%
share in some key supply-chain stages. Under this scenario, China's model companies
could leverage structural inference-cost advantages to capture a large share of global

token demand.
In a cost-performance scenario, China's model companies should have room to
gain share outside the most premium or frontier use cases.  China's models may
still lag global leaders in absolute intelligence but maintain a clear pricing advantage. We
believe this could be comparable to EVs, smartphones and home appliances, where
China's companies have gained about 30-50% share in select global categories and
markets by offering "good enough and lower-cost" products, supported by product

iteration, scale, channel expansion and improving brand recognition. Under this
scenario, we expect China's model companies to gain meaningful share in mid-range
and price-sensitive AI workloads, especially in high-volume use cases where frontier-
model performance is not essential.
In a performance- or ecosystem-led scenario, China's global share upside may
be capped if the intelligence gap remains meaningful.  If US frontier models
maintain a clear capability lead and AI  becomes embedded into enterprise workflows

(via AI agents), competition may shift from standalone model pricing to  ecosystem build-
out. We observe that global AI leaders are proactively moving in this direction: Anthropic
is expanding  from Claude Code into broader workflow products such as Claude
Cowork, Skills, MCP and enterprise plugins, while OpenAI is building Codex into a
developer and enterprise agent platform through Apps SDK, MCP integrations and
Codex plugins, according to the company. In this scenario, the user data and workflow

accumulations may become potential barriers to share shift, even if China's models offer
lower prices. We think the analogy could be closer to cloud or operating system, where
the global share of China's companies  remains in the low single digits to below 10%.
Under this scenario, China's model companies may still expand in select price-sensitive,
open-source or sovereign-AI markets, but their overall global revenue share may remain
relatively low. That said, China's AI labs are also actively building their own ecosystems,

with examples including Moonshot AI's Kimi Work integrating multiple productivity
applications, and Tencent opening WeChat's AI ecosystem capabilities to developers.

### Page 22

China Internet Sector 16 June 2026 ab 22
A potentially tiered global AI model market ahead
We see the cost-performance scenario as the more likely base case, as the global
model market is likely to become increasingly tiered, considering:
 B2B and B2C use cases have different capability requirements.  In B2B
scenarios, especially AI agents (e.g., AI coding), models need to handle more
complex workflows, tool use, long-context reasoning and error recovery. As these

use cases are directly linked to productivity improvement, we believe enterprise
users should be more willing to pay for stronger model capability. In contrast,
many B2C chatbot use cases are less complex, with users mainly requiring simple
Q&A and conversational interaction, making them less sensitive to incremental
frontier-model improvements, in our view.
 Agentic workloads are likely to be tiered by task complexity. Even within AI
agents, higher-end models may still be required for more difficult tasks (such as

project-level planning, architecture design and complex debugging), where
reasoning depth, planning capability and tool-use reliability matter more.
However, repetitive execution tasks (including routine code generation, document
processing, customer support and standardised enterprise operations) could be
served by lower-cost models once workflows become sufficiently structured, in our
view.
 Compute constraints could make frontier capacity a strategic scarce

resource. We believe this could further reinforce model tiering, as frontier AI
capability is not only driven by model algorithms, but also by a system-level
engineering stack across high-end GPUs and HBM, interconnect, data-centre
power availability and serving efficiency. With agent adoption driving much higher
inference intensity, customers may allocate premium models only to high-value
tasks where the incremental productivity gain justifies the cost, while routing

simpler and high-volume workloads to cheaper models running on lower-cost
hardware. In our view, this supports a multi-model market structure, with frontier
models priced as scarce intelligence capacity and cost-performance models serving
broader demand.
 High-end intelligence may not be suitable for unrestricted use. As models
become more capable in areas such as cybersecurity, autonomous tool use and
scientific reasoning, leading AI labs may need to apply stronger access controls and

safety restrictions. For example, Anthropic's Claude Mythos Preview was
reportedly limited to select trusted organisations, as the model demonstrated
advanced cybersecurity capabilities (e.g., identifying vulnerabilities in major
operating systems and browsers), raising potential misuse risks if widely released.
This suggests the most powerful models may not be universally available or
optimal for every user, further supporting a tiered market where frontier models

serve high-value, high-trust scenarios, while cost-performance models support
broader commercial adoption.
 Emerging AI gateways and model-routing platforms enable more efficient
model usage allocation. We also see the rise of model-routing platforms and AI
gateway services, such as OpenRouter, Not Diamond and similar services launched
by Tencent Cloud. These platforms can automatically match each query and task
with the most suitable model based on quality, cost, latency and reliability

requirements, which could support more tiered model offerings, in our view.
Shift from tokenmaxxing to ROI focus could benefit China's
models
We observe enterprise AI adoption shifting from tokenmaxxing towards ROI-
driven token allocation. In 2026, with the rise of AI coding agents such as Claude
Code and Codex, some enterprises may have used token usage dashboards or internal
leaderboards to encourage employee adoption. However, we have recently seen rising

market attention on token economics, as enterprises and model vendors increasingly
focus on whether incremental token consumption translates into measurable
productivity gains.
1. Enterprises are reassessing token usage incentives. Amazon reportedly  shut
down its internal KiroRank AI-token leaderboard after it encouraged employees to
inflate usage without necessarily solving business problems, while Meta's
employee-made “Claudeonomics” leaderboard was also reportedly   taken down.

This document is being provided for the exclusive use of RACHEL CHEN at MOUNTAIN HILL LTD.

### Page 23

China Internet Sector 16 June 2026 ab 23
Meanwhile, some enterprises have started to reassess AI tool subscriptions and
usage policies amid rising token bills.
2. Enterprise survey suggests low output conversion. Entelligence's May survey
of 2,444 enterprises suggests only 18% of enterprise AI coding spending is
converted into production output, with the remaining 82% absorbed by bug fixes,
code rewrites and review delays.
3. Token Expenditure Price Index has turned soft recently. SiliconData's LLM

Token Expenditure Index, an expenditure / usage-weighted average token price
index, could be viewed as a proxy for the market's marginal willingness to pay for
AI usage. The index rose sharply before May, likely driven by continued model
upgrades, rising AI agent usage and a favourable supply-demand backdrop, but
has weakened recently, potentially reflecting a shift from high-end closed-source
models towards more economical model choices.
Against this backdrop, we see China's model vendors as key beneficiaries of a

more ROI-focused token consumption trend.  As enterprise buyers become more
disciplined on token spending, cost efficiency should become a more important
purchasing factor, especially for high-volume workloads where frontier-model
performance is not essential. In our view, China's models are well positioned for this
shift, given their structurally lower inference costs, supported by model-architecture
innovation, continued engineering-efficiency improvements and resource / hardware

optimisation. This, along with the further narrowing model capability gap, should
support China models' global share-gain potential, in our view.
That said, we also acknowledge that geopolitical uncertainty may contain the
pace of overseas expansion, especially in more sensitive enterprise and government
use cases. However, we believe this could be partly offset by the well-diversified overseas
market opportunity. Apart from the US, other regions (such as Europe, Asia and the

Middle East) still lack strong local foundation-model presence, which should leave
meaningful room for China's model companies to compete.

China Internet Sector 16 June 2026 ab 25
WHAT'S PRICED IN?
China's AI foundation model companies have delivered strong share
performances since their listings in January, despite some recent moderation. For
instance,  MiniMax have risen 214% as of 15 June 2026.  We think this was mainly driven
by rising revenue growth expectations (e.g.,  MiniMax's management set internal targets
of US$1bn ARR by end-2026, compared to ~US150m in Feb 2026), as investors see

rising token demand amid the rapid scaling of AI agent products across OpenClaw and
AI coding agents. Meanwhile, continued model iteration and capital-market updates
(e.g., HSCI and HSTECH inclusion; proposed A-share listings) are additional boosts to
share performance, in our view.
While investor attention on AI foundation model companies remains high, we think
investors also recognise that China model stocks' current valuations are at
elevated levels versus global peers (e.g., OpenAI and Anthropic are valued at below

30x P/ARR in the private market). Investors are likely to continue monitoring potential
catalysts over the next few months, including  model upgrades, IPO lock-up expiry, Stock
Connect inclusion,  progress on A-share listings as well as global AI leaders'  listing in US
market.
That said, we believe the market still underestimates China model companies'
cost-performance advantages and their long-term upside potential from global
share gains. We expect global investors to increasingly recognise China AI models'

cost-efficiency advantage, especially as the market shifts from pursuing token-usage
quantity (i.e., “tokenmaxxing”) towards token-usage quality, with more emphasis on
ROI. Meanwhile, as the capability gap with US models continues to narrow and China's
models maintain structural inference-pricing advantages, we believe they could
accelerate global AI penetration and support China AI models' global share gains.
Despite the premium valuations, we anticipate significant, long-term growth upside

ahead for China's leading  model companies, which may help digest China AI model
companies'  (e.g., MiniMax)  valuation multiples as revenue scaling crystallises.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We believe key sector risks include: 1) an evolving competitive landscape and intensifying
competition; 2) fast-moving trends in technology, as well as internet users' needs and
preferences; 3) uncertain monetisation; 4) the rising cost of traffic acquisition, content and
brand promotions; 5) the upkeep of IT systems; 6) expansion into international markets; 7)
adverse changes in market sentiment; and 8) regulatory risks.

Tencent: Our price target is based on sum of the parts (SOTP). We believe the key risks for
Tencent include: 1) an evolving and intensifying competitive landscape; 2) new business
execution; 3) integration of invested companies and businesses; 4) rising costs of traffic
acquisition, content and brand promotions; 5) IT system upkeep; 6) expansion into
international markets; 7) infringement of intellectual property rights; 8) departures of key
management; and 9) regulatory risks.

Alibaba: Our price target is based on SOTP. We believe the key risks for Alibaba are: 1)
regulatory changes, particularly for data usage and online content; 2) Chinese and global
macroeconomic headwinds; 3) competitive pressure from traditional offline retailers; 4)
interruptions of information technology and systems; 5) short-term profitability pressure from
long-term investment; 6) execution and management complexity resulting from its multiple

platforms; 7) corporate governance – significant voting control by the Alibaba Partnership;
and 8) the loss of the services of founder Jack Ma.
MiniMax: We adopt Price/ARR as our valuation methodology for MiniMax. Key downside risks
include: macro and geopolitical uncertainties that may constrain AI adoption; competitive
and operational challenges delaying monetisation and pressuring margins; data-related
model training risks; user-generated content governing risks; and disruption of key service

providers.
Key upside risks include: easing geopolitical tensions and export restrictions that may improve
access to advanced hardware and accelerate model upgrades; technological breakthroughs
in AI training and infrastructure efficiency that could enhance model capability and margins;
and faster user adoption and commercialisation driving stronger monetisation and
profitability.
Baidu: We derive our price target for Baidu using a sum-of-the-parts valuation methodology.

We believe the key risks for Baidu include: 1) an evolving and intensifying competitive
landscape; 2) execution of new businesses; 3) integration of invested companies and
businesses; 4) rising cost of traffic acquisitions, content and brand promotions; 5) maintaining
and upgrading IT systems; 6) infringement of intellectual property rights; 7) expansion into
international markets; 8) key management departures; and 9) regulatory risks.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

would be consistent with its investment objective and other principal investment strategies. The returns on a portfolio incorporating ESG factors or Sustainable Investing
considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the
investment opportunities available to such portfolios may differ.

新應材 4749 TT  ( 電子–半導體 ) 
垂直整合奠定長期競爭力 
投資與評價 
1. 2Q26 營收符合研究部先前預估，油價上漲影響顯示器業務利潤率表現
使毛利率季減。 
2. 展望 3Q26，預估在先進製程投片量逐漸成長下，營收將呈現季增趨
勢，毛利率因顯示器業務獲利率回穩、規模經濟效益發揮亦將季增。 
3. 隨 N3/N2 等新世代節點比例逐漸提升，新應材成長走勢也將更貼近大
客戶先進製程的推進節奏。研究部預估 2H26 營收將呈現逐季走高。 
4. 預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 15.42 元/23.17 元，YoY＋40.0%/+50.3%，
具有長期投資價值。研究部維持買進投資建議，惟考量近期市場波動，
將目標價調整至 850 元，以 37x FY27 獲利預估。 
重點更新 
2H26 營收獲利將呈現季季高，成長走勢逐漸貼近大客戶先進製程趨勢 
展望 2H26，研究部認為隨 N3/N2 等新世代節點比例逐漸提升，其放量效應
將更完整展現在整體營收與獲利表現上，成長走勢也將更貼近大客戶先進製
程的推進節奏。研究部預估營收將呈現逐季走高。獲利率方面，2Q26 受到油
價波動些微侵蝕顯示器業務獲利率，3Q26 原物料成本管理控制有成，顯示
器業務獲利率將逐漸回穩。 
新應材、新寶紘、昱鐳應材各擁優勢 
新應材目前正積極向先進製程與先進封裝兩大方向拓展產品版圖。 新應材亦
開始與策略夥伴新寶紘、昱鐳應材 （7932 TT） 攜手，分別鎖定不同站點協同
布局：新寶紘除既有產品外，新增翹曲控制膜與離形膜，聚焦於翹曲控制與
保護；昱鐳應材則由既有CCL 樹脂延伸至 Underfill、CPO、TGV 等新產品。
透過三方資源整合 ， 綜效逐漸顯現 ， 產品版圖持續由既有優勢領域向外延伸 。 
垂直整合奠定長期競爭力 
新應材與光阻劑日廠具有不同商業模式。以日廠信越化學為例，上游單體材
料由大阪有機、東洋合成等多家日商供應，有別於日本供應鏈將原料、純化、
配方由不同企業分工的模式，新應材具備垂直整合優勢，從原料至配方開發
皆自主掌握，在晶圓廠客戶對於即時服務要求逐漸提升，新應材可加快研發
與客製化開發進度，將彰顯出新應材之競爭優勢。 
王筠婷 / 特用化學、半導體週邊 
+886-2-2326-9899#7961 
vanessawang@cathayfut.com.tw

### Page 2

訪談內容 
預估 3Q26 營收獲利重回季增軌道 
2Q26 營收為 12.33 億元(QoQ-1.0%/YoY+6.9%)，符合研究部先前預估的 11.94 億元。營收些
微季減主要係因於 4 月因面臨客戶海外廠提前至 Q1 拉貨，且無客戶新廠洗線收入；毛利率優於
我們原先預估的 42.7%，達 44.5%(QoQ-0.9 ppts/ YoY-1.7 ppts)，油價上漲影響顯示器業務利
潤率表現使毛利率季減；EPS 達 3.28 元(QoQ-10.4%/ YoY+7.0%)，符合我們原先預估的 3.32
元。 
展望 3Q26，8 月因客戶海外廠新廠洗線帶動化學品需求，月營收明顯月增至 4.9 億元，研究部
預估在先進製程投片量逐漸成長下，3Q26 營收將呈現季增趨勢，研究部預估將達 13.28 億元
(QoQ+7.8%/YoY+24.7%)。預估毛利率因顯示器業務獲利率回穩、半導體業務規模經濟效益發
揮，季增至 45.8%(QoQ+1.3 ppts/ YoY+3.6 ppts)，EPS 為 3.97 元(QoQ+21.1%/ YoY+27.0%)。

表一. 2Q26 財報與原先預期對比                                       (單位﹔百萬元)

資料來源：新應材，國泰證期研究部整理

2H26 營收獲利將呈現季季高，成長走勢逐漸貼近大客戶先進製程趨勢 
展望 2H26，研究部認為新應材成長動能來自於大客戶兩座 N2 新廠於今年投產，Rinse、BARC、
EBR 三項核心產品出貨量將隨 N2 產能爬坡逐季走高。由於新應材產品線橫跨 N40 至 N2 等全
部製程節點，過去在新舊製程產能互相消長下，整體成長力道容易被稀釋，難以顯著反映如 N2
單一世代放量的貢獻，隨 N3/N2 等新世代節點比例逐漸提升，其放量效應將更完整展現在整體
營收與獲利表現上，成長走勢也將更貼近大客戶先進製程的推進節奏。研究部預估營收將呈現逐
季走高。 
獲利率方面 ，2Q26 受到油價波動些微侵蝕顯示器業務獲利率 ，3Q26 原物料成本管理控制有成 ，
顯示器業務獲利率將逐漸回穩。在半導體業務方面，隨Rinse 出貨規模隨客戶整體產能成長而擴
大，剛打入 N2 製程的 BARC、EBR 將隨 N2 產能快速爬坡而同步放量 ， 毛利率亦有望逐步走升 。
整體而言，規模經濟效益將於 2026 年逐步顯現，預期 FY26 毛利率亦將逐季提升，成長幅度將
較營收成長更為顯著。 預估FY26 全年營收達 52.6 億元(YoY+23.4%)，毛利率達 45.4%(YoY+2.3 
ppts)，全年 EPS 為 15.42 元(YoY+37.0%)，全年獲利達歷史新高。

### Page 3

新應材、新寶紘、昱鐳應材各擁優勢 
新應材深耕黃光微影製程領域多年，目前正積極向先進製程與先進封裝兩大方向拓展產品版圖。
先進製程材料方面，至未來新製程節點將逐漸有新產品進入供應鏈。KrF/ArF 光阻劑研發進度符
合預期，可望切入A10 製程應用；既有 BARC 產品亦朝下一世代升級，除已放量的傳統版本外，
新一代產品將加入耐乾／濕蝕刻、高深寬比結構填孔及平坦化能力，使BARC 功能由單純抗反射
層，進一步延伸為兼具蝕刻保護與底層平坦化的複合型材料，目前亦正在驗證中。 
在先進封裝領域，公司產品布局已由前段特化材料延伸至晶圓薄化、切割及暫時鍵合等後段關鍵
製程，包括背面研磨保護層、雷射開槽電漿切割保護層、暫時鍵合膠等新品。新應材亦開始與策
略夥伴新寶紘、昱鐳應材（7932 TT）攜手合作，三方分別鎖定不同製程節點協同布局：新寶紘
除研磨膠帶、切割膠帶等既有產品外，新增翹曲控制膜（WCF）與離形膜（Release Film），聚焦
於載板翹曲控制與保護應用；昱鐳應材則由既有 CCL 樹脂延伸至 Underfill、CPO、TGV 及 Wafer 
Sealing Resin 等新產品，切入封裝填膠、光通訊與玻璃基板等領域。整體而言，新應材透過三
方資源整合、各自攻佔不同製程站點的分工布局，前段微影材料與後段封裝材料的綜效逐漸顯現，
產品版圖持續由既有優勢領域向外延伸。

垂直整合奠定長期競爭力 
新應材與光阻劑日廠具有不同商業模式。以日廠信越化學為例，上游單體材料由大阪有機、東洋
合成等多家日商供應，有別於日本供應鏈將原料、純化、配方由不同企業分工的模式，新應材具
備垂直整合優勢，從原料至配方開發皆自主掌握，在晶圓廠客戶對於即時服務要求逐漸提升，新
應材可加快研發與客製化開發進度，將彰顯出新應材之競爭優勢。 
研究部預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 15.42 元/23.17 元，YoY＋40.0%/+50.3%，新應材目前交易於
47/31 倍之 2026/2027 年獲利預估，過去歷史本益比區間為 50X~75X，具有長期投資價值。研
究部維持買進投資建議 ，惟考量近期市場波動 ，將目標價調整至 850 元 ， 以37x FY27 獲利預估 。

圖 1. 新應材產品研發計畫

資料來源：新應材、國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 4

產業概況 
光阻周邊市場份額集中度高，台廠迎來新契機 
隨製程越演先進，在半導體微影製程中除了光阻劑本身外，另有一系列光阻周邊材料扮演關鍵角
色，包括表面改質劑 （Rinse） 、洗邊劑 （EBR） 、底部抗反射層 （BARC） 、頂部抗反射層 （TARC）、
稀釋劑 （Thinner） 以及光阻剝除劑 （Stripper） 等。根據研調機構Deloitte 及 WSTS 資料顯示，
2025 年全球黃光微影材料中，光阻及光阻周邊市場規模達67 億美元，預估至2030 年市場規模
將進一步成長至 96 億美元。除光阻劑市場規模增長，製程節點逐漸縮小，先進製程的高良率要
求亦推升對 Rinse、BARC 等提升良率材料的用量 ， 對於光阻周邊材料的材料精密度亦有所提升 。
光阻周邊材料市場集中度高 ， 主要由少數日 、 美 、 德大廠壟斷主要份額 ， 國外廠商如美商Merck、
美商杜邦、日商東京應化等。但在半導體在地化趨勢下，亦為台廠帶來新契機，相關廠商如新應
材(4749 TT)。

公司簡介 
新應材為台廠唯一光阻周邊產品供應商 
新應材股份有限公司成立於 2003 年，由面板光阻材料及清洗劑起家，自 2018 年開始轉型半導
體材料開發，聚焦對良率至關重要的微影（Lithography）特化材料，為台灣唯一在半導體微影
製程從原料合成、純化到配方自主設計，並持續擴大量產的特化材料廠。 
目前業務可分為 1) 半導體特化材料 ： 產品包括微影製程所需的表面改質劑(Rinse)、 洗邊劑(EBR)、 
清洗劑(Cleaner)、 顯影劑(Developer)、 底部抗反射層(BARC)等 ， 另有先進封裝材料及光學元件 。
2) 顯示器特化材料：產品包括顯示器用光阻劑、高/低折射率材料等。2024 年半導體特化材料
營收占比約八成，顯示器特化材料約兩成。 
生產基地方面，公司研發總部位於桃園龍潭，並在台南及高雄設有生產基地。台南廠為配方廠，
高雄一期則為合成及純化製程，高雄二期則涵蓋前段合成及純化以及後段的配方製程。

圖 2. 光阻領域各產品之供應商

資料來源：新應材，國泰證期研究部整理

### Page 5

ESG 政策 
新應材由於 2024 年尚未達 20 億台幣資本額，目前尚未受到強制規範出具 ESG 報告書，預計將
於 2025 年 8 月開始出具 ESG 報告書。

⚫ 環境安全政策 
因產品、活動、服務過程中對人員健康安全危害及所產生的污染與能、資源之耗用對環境產生了
衝擊，為善盡社會之責任，我們將致力於危害、污染預防及持續改善，以降低各項製程及產品對
環境的危害，新應材公司已取得 ISO 45001 職安衛管理系統與 ISO 14001 環境管理系統認證，
定期執行內外部稽核，持續改善。

⚫ 節能減排政策 
新應材制定聚醯亞胺(PI) 溶液回收計劃。分離廢液中的 PI 及溶劑並同時再生及重覆使用，朝向
零液體排放前進 ， 大量減少焚燒處理後產生大量溫室氣體對環境的衝擊問題以及減少石化資源無
法再生循環使用的問題。新應材已榮獲重要晶圓代工客戶頒發「供應商節能減碳輔導專案」感謝
狀。

⚫ 綠色產品政策 
新應材為確保公司在全球供應鏈中不支持或助長任何形式的武裝衝突和人權侵犯 ， 並促進負責任
的採購行為。新應材承諾不使用並要求所有相關供應商配合禁用「來自衝突區域的金屬礦產」 。
新應材將對所有相關供應商進行盡職調查，確保其遵守新應材的政策。對於揭露資訊不完整或是
來自非合格熔煉廠者，新應材將持續督促其改善，並且不排除尋找其他供應商作為替代方案。

貨櫃 
長榮 (2603 TT)

重新覆蓋  
中立(Neutral) 
短線運價上漲，長線仍有回落風險  
⚫ 2Q26 EPS 7.41 元， 低於 市場預期  
⚫ 預期運價 3Q~4Q26 仍有支撐  
⚫ 2027 年運力將 大幅增加，運價或 將面臨壓力  
2Q26 EPS 7.41 元，獲利較同期成長主因 運價提升 ： 長榮 2Q26 營收
1,051 億(YoY +21.6 %)，毛利率 22.8 % (YoY +1.2 ppts )，淨利 160.3 億
(YoY+43.9%) ，EPS 7.41 元低於市場預期的 7.89 元。營收及獲利較同
期成長 主要受惠 自 2Q26 起貨櫃運價 走揚 ，主因歐美貨 主為因應美國
附加關稅即將到期而提前備貨引發的搶運潮、歐美國家在包含 AI 相
關硬體設備及傳統民生消費品的實質回補庫存訂單湧現、巴拿馬運河
因持續面臨缺水問題實施降載與限制通行艘數、及三月地緣衝突導致
燃油價格飆漲促使航商將成本壓力轉嫁至遠洋線運價等多重原因 ，帶
動亞洲至北美、歐洲等主要航線的需求 在 2Q26 轉強。  
運 價 預期至少 可 持續 至 3Q26 ， 年 底 運 價或 將回落： 近期 的貨 櫃整 體
運價維持高檔整理，若分航線來看，美洲線受惠於北美零售商提前備
貨旺季、鎖定關稅變數的搶運潮，加上航商持續控艙，美西 及美東航
線運價維持強勢 ，歐洲與地中海航線因歐陸整體消費復甦力道較弱 ，
運價持續小幅修正 。展望後市，在第三季傳統出貨旺季與紅海短期 仍
未全面復 航的雙重疊加下，市場實質運力持續受限，預估高檔運價至
少能維持至 9~10 月，帶動 3Q26 單季營運呈現價量齊揚，若塞港與船
舶調度緊繃等供給端瓶頸遲未緩解，整體運價的支撐力道更可望進一
步延續至年底。 惟長期來看貨櫃航運市場依舊未能擺脫供過於求的結
構性問題，加上中東衝突若進一步 緩解 可能引發 更多航商跟進 紅海航
道復航，市場運價在 2027 年後 將面臨回落壓力 。 
中長期運價面臨修正風險 ， 建議區間操作 ：綜合上述 ， 我們預估2026/27
年營收 4,653 /3,944 億(YoY+22.8 %/-15.2 %)，EPS 3 7.13 /19.29 元， 以
9/11 收盤價計算，目前本淨比約在我們預估 2027 年淨值的 0.82 倍，
考量 Q3 旺季效應消退後， 在 2027 年全球運力大幅提升情況下運價仍
有回落壓力 ，評價短期內難有上修空間，故給予中立評等，目標價 257
元(2027 年每股 淨值 *0.9 倍本淨比 )。 
風險因子 ：地緣政治 ；運力供需 。

 公 司 簡 介 ： 長榮海運（ 2603 ）成立於 1968 年 ， 為 台 灣 規 模 最 大 的 貨 櫃
航 運 業 者 ， 經 營 超 過 200 艘 全 貨 櫃 輪 。 公 司 營 運 網 絡 涵 蓋 全 球 五 大 洲 ，
主力航線橫跨遠東至北美、遠東至歐洲、地中海及亞洲近洋等主要貿易
通 道。長 榮 近 年 積 極 優 化 船 隊 結 構，導 入 24,000 TEU （ A 級 船 ） 與15,000 
TEU （ F 級 船 ） 等 大 箱 位 、 低 耗 能 之 巨 型 貨 櫃 船 ， 以 實 現 規 模 經 濟 並 降
低每  TEU 運 力 之 平 均 營 運 成 本 。 目 前 地 中 海 航 運 （ MSC ） 、 馬 士 基
（ Maersk ） 與 達 飛 海 運 （ CMA CGM ） 等 前 三 大 航 商 合 計 已 掌 控 全 球 近
五成的總運力，長榮則全球排名第 7 名  
圖表 一 、 長榮 貨 櫃 運 力 排 名 全球第 7

資料來源：公司  
2025 年 海 運 聯 盟 改 組 ， 長 榮 屬 海 洋 聯 盟： 全球貨櫃海運市場在 2025 年
經重大的聯盟結構洗牌，隨著 2M 聯 盟 正 式 解 散 ，全 球 運 力 榜 首 地 中 海
航運改以獨立營運，近年透過大舉造船與收購二手船極速擴張，已具備
無 需 依 賴 聯 盟 即 可 獨 自 覆 蓋 全 球 航 線 的 龐 大 運 力 ； 馬 士 基 與 赫 伯 羅 特
（ Hapag -Lloyd ） 則 共 組 「 雙 子 星 聯 盟 （Gemini Coopera tion ）」 ，特色
為揚棄傳統大型船隻多點直航，改採軸輻式轉運架構，將運力集中於雙
方具備掌控權的幾座核心樞紐港口 ；海 洋 網 聯 船 務 （ONE ） 、陽 明 與HMM
則轉型為「卓越聯盟（ Premier Alliance ）」 ， 為 填 補 赫 伯 羅 特 出 走 後 的
運力缺口，採取更為彈性的對外合作策略，與獨立營運的 MSC 在亞歐
航 線 達 成 艙 位 交 換 合 作 。 長 榮 則 持 續 隸 屬 於 「 海 洋 聯 盟 （ Ocean 
Alliance ） 」 ， 與 達 飛 海 運 、 中 遠 海 控 （COSCO ）及東方海外（ OOCL ）
深化合作，並已簽署協議將聯盟合作期限延長至 2032 年 ， 主 打 高 度 的
穩定性與廣泛的點對點直航，在北美與歐洲等東西向主幹線擁有全球最
高的市佔率與最密集的班次網路。

圖表二、海運盟經過多次重組，現為三大聯盟 +MSC 的格局

資料來源： PFE express  
航 商 長 期 繞 航 好 望 角 ， 部 份 航 商近期 復 航 紅 海 ： 自紅海地緣政治衝突爆
發以來，全球主要航商為維護船舶與海員安全，大量將亞歐及地中海航
線繞道非洲好望角，導致單趟航程延長 10 ~15 天 ， 有 效 吸 收 了 全 球 市 場
的 過剩 運 力 ， 故 紅 海 全 面 復 航 通 常 被 視 為 運 價 見 頂 暴 跌 的 最 大 利 空 風
險。近期已有部分航商如馬士基等，在 評 估 相 關 安 全 性 及 獲 利 後 ， 嘗 試
性重啟部分紅海及蘇伊士運河航線，儘管少數航商開始恢復部分紅海運
力，但全球絕大部分主力船隊目前仍維持繞航策略，紅海復航進度與安
全風險依然是影響短期市場運力供給的重要變數 ， 惟 是 否 短 期 大 幅 影 響
運價，仍需視復航速度。  
圖表三、繞航好望角吸收全球航運運力，有助運價獲得支撐

資料來源： Czapp

4 
2027 年 運 力 將 大 幅 增 加 ， 運 價 或 將 面 臨 壓 力 ：在中長期運力供需結構方
面， Alphaliner 等 國 際 海 運 調 研 機 構 普 遍 預 估 2027 年 全 球 貨 櫃 海 運 將 面
臨較大的供過於求壓力，因先前新船訂單陸續交付， 2027 年 預 計 將 有 高
達 280 萬 TEU 的新船運力集中投入市場，推升 2027 年 全 球 運 力 供 給 年
增率達到 7.7% ~8.1% ， 在 總 體 經 濟 成 長 平 緩 的 基 準 假 設 下 ， 全 球 貨 櫃 貨
物需求年增率預計僅維持在 2.5%~3.0% 的 區 間 ， 在 供 給 成 長 速 度 為 需
求增速 2 倍 以 上 的 顯 著 失 衡 下 ， 若 缺 乏 其 他 不 可 抗 力 事 件 被 動 消 化 運
力，運價或將面臨下行修正壓力。  
中 長 期 海 運 市 場 仍 面 臨 供 過 於 求 ， 建 議 區 間 操 作 ：在 評 價 面 上 ， 因 馬 士
基於最近一次財報會議中提出較樂觀觀點，認為全球供應鏈瓶頸與塞港
問題在「港口基礎建 設不足」下將趨於常態化，長期將吸收多餘運力，
進而支撐運價維持在高於疫情前的合理水準，成為海運股近期 P/B 評價
區間上修的依據。然而，調研機構也多次在報告中指出近期碼頭擁擠的
最大 原 因 在 於 紅 海 危 機 繞 道 打 亂 了 班 表 ， 航 班 凖 點 率 大 降 ， 導 致 原 本 應
該分批抵達的船隻在同一時間塞進港，一旦航商適應新的航線節奏或事
件落幕，港口壅塞通常能在幾個月內迅速緩解， 在 未 來 幾 年 有 大 量 船 隻
超額供給的情況下恐不足以 成 為 支 撐 長 期 運 價 的 常 態 理 由， 整 體 而 言 ，
我們 認為 海 運 股 評 價 處 於 「 塞 港 常 態 化 支 撐 」 與 「 長 期 供 給 過 剩 風 險 」
兩大力量 交錯 的 評 價 調 整 期 。  
圖表 四 、 季 度 財 報 與 預 估 差 異

資料來源： Cmoney 、 Bloomberg ， 中 信 投 顧 整 理

營業比重(2Q26)

個股與大盤走勢(%)

張敦翔 
jason.chang@ctbcsis.com

京鼎 (3413 TT)

維持評等  
買進(Buy) 
期待展望指引確實兌現  
⚫ 2Q26 獲利表現明顯不如預期，然 2H26 可望逐步改善  
⚫ 斥資泰國新 廠擴建因應 2028 年後客 戶需求  
⚫ 維持買進 建議 係因積極展望 真實 、 評價位居 低檔 綜合考量  
2Q26 財報 獲利 遜於 預期 ： 京鼎 2Q26 營收 達 63 億元 (QoQ +13%、
YoY+ 21%)， 季增 主 因 製造服務 客戶 拉貨 動能 續強、及富蘭登航太專
案貢獻 ； 惟 毛 利 率 受 制 產 品 組 合 及 春 武 里 廠 量 產 初 期 成 本 影 響僅
22.3% ，營益率 12.2% ，單季 EPS 為 4.61 元， 均遜於 預期。  
3Q26 保持季增趨勢，預期 2H26 毛利率逐步回升 ：展望後市，管理層
看好 AI 持續推動薄膜沉積、蝕刻、檢測設備等主力產品需求成長，
預期 3Q26 營收 維持季增趨勢。此外，公司也認為 2Q26 毛利率將為全
年谷底， 並於 2H26 迎來回升，主因 (1) 春武里廠 一期 已於 7M26 開始
挹注，規模效益可望 加速 顯現，並維持今年底 ~明年初獲利 轉 正 預 期、
(2) 檢測客戶業務亦從原先 direct buy 模式轉變 為部分關鍵 零部件由客
戶 consign ，雖會使營收量體減少，但對公司獲利 率改善 具 一定 助 益。 
訂單能見度 歷史新高 ，加速投產泰國新廠因應強勁需求 ：目前公司整
體訂單能見度已達 9 個月，部分產品更已看到 2028 年，也驅動當前
各區廠 房稼 動 率均接近 、或已經 滿載 。而 為因應 客 戶 積極 forecast ，
京鼎甫於 8 月斥資 2.34 億美元投資春武里廠二期 ，以提前布局 2028
年後客戶需求。產能、時程規劃 方面，目前春武里一期約占 15% 產能，
預期明年中 再擴至 25%，且二期規模則約與一期相當，並 預期 於 2H27
隨設備進機逐步開出產能 ，以平衡 新廠折舊 對毛利率 的稀釋 程度 。 
獲利待實際成果檢視，惟因積極展望 /評 價 位 階 仍 維 持「 買 進 」評 等：
我們認為， 雖 2H26 毛利率指引回升、春武里二期生產效率改善速度
優於一期、及客戶訂單持續驅動營收規模成長，均有利京鼎 未來 獲利
表現保持穩健，但 考量 2Q26 最終獲利表現明顯不如預期，故 仍需 檢
視後續季度 實際財報成果。 惟基於 大客戶及 京鼎 自身積極擴產展望，
我們認同半導體需求仍真實且強勁 ，且 公司 評價也位居低檔，故維持
對其 「買進」 評等， 目標價則調整為 395 元(2027 年 EPS*13.5 倍 PER)。 
風險因子 ：毛利率遲遲無法轉佳 。

202 6 /09 /11

1

前日收盤價 295 元

鄭子凡 
Jasmin.cheng@ctbcsis.com

被 動 元 件 
信昌電(6173 TT)         法說/座談摘要 
信昌電 下半年將漲價 30% ，AI 需求仍暢旺  
⚫ AI 電 源需求強勁，高階產品放量  
⚫ 大尺寸供給 吃緊，下半年漲價約 30% 
⚫ 2027 年雙廠投 產， MLCC 與 高階粉末產能 擴增  
結論: 
我們認為信昌電受惠 AI Server PSU 需求成長，NP0、Mega Cap 等高階
產品持續放量，加上大尺寸特殊品供給吃緊及漲價效益，將同步推升營
收與毛利率。產能方面，楊梅二廠及六甲粉末廠均預計於 2027 年 7 月
投產，MLCC 產能可望接近倍增、NP0 產能增加 60%～70%，高階粉末
產能則提升約 50%，有助支應後續 AI 電源及高功率應用需求。隨產品
放量 、 價格提升及擴產效益逐步發酵 ， 我們正向看待公司後續營運表現 。

一、 公司簡介 
⚫ 信昌電成立於 1990 年，為國內少數能自行供給瓷粉原料並同
時銷售積層陶瓷電容的被動元件廠商，更是唯一有能力由上
游初發原料，向下垂直整合至被動晶片元件的廠商。2005 年
與華新集團進行策略聯盟、2008 年合併弘電電子，將銷售範
圍從介電瓷粉 、 半導性陶瓷電容器瓷片 、 積層陶瓷電容 、 晶片
電阻延伸到二極體與線圈，成為國內唯一可全數提供特殊電
容 、 電感 、 電阻之被動元件供應商 ， 公司亦為國內唯一擁有自
製 介 電 瓷 粉 之 晶 片 電 容 器 廠 家 。 公 司 主 要 競 爭 者 為 大 毅
(2478)、禾伸堂(3026)、Kyocera、Vishay、TDK、Murata 等。 
圖表一、全球營運據點

資料來源:信昌電、中國信託整理

 二、 財務狀況 
⚫ 2Q26 營收 13.76 億元，季增 35.7%，年增 25.2%，主要受惠 AI 需求快
速成長，終端客戶於 2Q26 由觀望轉為積極拉貨，帶動營收明顯成長。  
⚫ 2Q26 毛利率 28.84%，季增 0.49ppts，年增 5.24ppts，主要受惠 AI 大尺
寸產品出貨增加，產品組合優化；去年同期則以車用產品為主。 
⚫ 2Q26 稅前淨利約 3.18 億元，季增 49%、年增約 598%。去年同期業外
受到匯率等因素影響，基期相對較低；今年則因匯率影響較為有利，使
業外表現同步改善。 
⚫ 2Q26 EPS 1.79 元。 
⚫ 2023 年主要受到終端庫存調整影響，歷經約 8 個季度的去庫存；
2024 年起電動車應用快速發展，公司亦持續布局 Power 相關產
品，並於 3Q25 開始明顯受惠 AI Server Power 需求爆發。  
圖表一、營收、毛利率與營益率趨勢

資料來源: 信昌電、中信投顧整理 
三、 產品占比 
⚫ 1H26 依產品=MLCC 68%、Powder 12%、Chip-resister 16%、Inductor 
2%、Others 2%，公司目前的營收主力仍集中在 MLCC 產品，且主要聚
焦於大尺寸、高壓及高階 MLCC。 
⚫ 截至 2026 年 7 月依應用=工業 5%、網通 25%、消費 5%、特殊 5%、車
用 13%，過去工業應用占比約四成，經過近四年的產品布局與市場結構
調整，目前已提升至約 53%。圖表二、公司 AI 占比變化。 
⚫ 工業應用包含 AI Power 與 Robot，其中 AI 營收占比由 2024 年約 2%～
3%升至 2025 年 6%，2026 年至今達約 9%，預期下半年有望突破

10%、最高達 16%。機器人主要應用於減速器及控制系統，營收占比由
2024 年 1%～2%升至 2025 年近 3%，今年持續成長；惟大規模需求預
計 2027～2028 年發酵，短期仍以 AI 為主要動能。

四、 營運概況 
⚫ 2026 年 7 月後進行減資，目前實收資本額約 16.09 億元，較過去減少約
1 億元。 
⚫ 公司過去積極布局車用市場，2023 年起與中國 Tier-1 客戶合作，2024
年開始貢獻營收。受惠中國電動車市場成長及政策刺激，車用營收占比
由過去約 5%～6%提升至 14%～15%，目前約 13%，後續預期維持穩定
基本盤。此外，公司持續布局自動駕駛及車用無線充電等新應用，若未
來滲透率提升，有望成為車用業務新的成長動能。 
⚫ AI 目前主要用料銷售增長趨勢包含 Mega Cap+LLC(NP0)，其中 LC 自
2024 年起快速成長，2Q26 需求更明顯放大，Mega Cap 亦維持成長。隨
AI 產品占比提升，亦有助於改善產品組合及毛利率。公司於 2024 年下
半年開始切入 AI 相關應用，且積極進行新產品開發與客戶 design-in 
⚫ 公司能持續切入 AI 及高階市場，主要仰賴三項能力(1)粉末技術：掌握
高階、細粉及瓷化材料，並布局微波通訊、LTCC 及 LEO 相關材料。
(2)自動化能力。(3)製程能力：高階及客製化產品需頻繁調整，公司具
備快速修改製程、參數及導入新設計的能力。 
⚫ 公司產品應用由消費電子、 4G 及一般伺服器網通，逐步拓展至
AI Server 、AI Power 、 人形/協作機器人及低軌衛星。展望未來三

年， 2026 ～2027 年以 AI Power 為主要動能， 2027 ～2028 年則看
好人形機器人接棒，低軌衛星則屬中長期潛在市場。  
⚫ AI Server Power 相關產品目前成長最明顯，主要產品包括 Mega Cap 及
NP0 等高階 MLCC，其中 Mega Cap ASP 約為一般產品 7～8 倍。 
⚫ Mega Cap 可降低裂紋/短路風險、改善散熱、降低 ESR/ESL 並節省 PCB
空間。 
⚫ AI Server 單機價值高，客戶更重視可靠度及安全性，帶動高階特殊
MLCC 採用。 
⚫ >1206 尺寸 MLCC 數量占比僅約 1.49%，但受惠 AI Server/Power 高壓、
高功率需求成長，憑藉高 ASP 及公司逾 30 年技術與台達、光寶等客戶
基礎，價值量成長潛力高。 
⚫ 電容約占被動元件市場 82%，其中 MLCC 約占電容 2/3；大尺寸
MLCC 數量占比僅約 1.5%，但價值占比可達 20%～30%，高階產品價
值量明顯較高。 
圖表三、2025 年 MLCC 尺寸使用量

資料來源:信昌電、中國信託整理 
⚫ 2018 年 MLCC 缺貨主要是一般品全面缺貨；本次 AI 需求則集中在大尺
寸、高壓、高階特殊 MLCC，因供應商少、技術及認證門檻高，新增產
能需要時間，因此公司認為供給缺口有望延續較長時間。 
⚫ 大尺寸特殊品因供應商少、技術及品質門檻高，目前台灣主要由少數廠
商供應，公司與 H 公司為主要供應商之一，短期新增供給仍有限，公司
認為此波缺貨有機會延續約 3 年。

【中信投顧投資分析報告】

5 
⚫ 公司具備粉末到元件的垂直整合能力，六甲粉末廠主要布局高階粉末，
除了供應自有 MLCC，也可對外銷售。此外，粉末技術亦延伸至 TCC
／LTCC、低軌衛星摻雜材料及高階微波粉末，可作為未來中長期成長
方向。 
五、 公司產能 
⚫ 目前主要生產據點包括桃園及楊梅，其中桃園廠主要負責粉末及電阻相
關產品，楊梅廠則為公司主要的 MLCC 生產基地。 
⚫ 楊梅二廠: 今年投入約 15 億元 ， 主要生產高壓低損耗 、 高溫穩定型MLCC
及 AI Server 相關產品。受 AI 需求帶動，高階 MLCC 訂單快速增加，預
計 2027 年 7 月量產。 
⚫ 六甲粉末廠：去年投入約12 億元建置新粉末廠，導入高度自動化並提升
細緻、高階粉末生產技術，預計2027 年下半年量產，提升高階粉末自製
能力。 
⚫ 今年楊梅一廠產能較去年約增加 30%；明年楊梅二廠開出後，整體產能
預計較目前增加近一倍，粉末產能亦同步增加約50%。其中 NP0 產能明
年預計較目前增加約 60%～70%。 
⚫ 公司善用集團既有廠房 ， 採租賃方式快速擴產 ， 因此建廠速度較同業快 ，
具備一定競爭優勢。 
六、 未來展望 
⚫ 展望 2H26，公司預期營運仍將維持穩健成長，下半年主要成長動能包括
漲價效益、產能擴充、高階產品放量。 
⚫ 未來毛利率有望再往上突破。 
七、 Q & A 
⚫ 2Q26 AI Power 營收佔比 9.2%，主要產品為何？Mega Cap 是否開始放
量？ 
- 9.2%指的是 AI Power 產品佔公司總營業額的比重，由於信昌電的電
容（MLCC） 產品約佔整體營收的60%多，若單純以電容產品線作為
分母計算，AI 相關產品的營收佔比實際上會高於 9.2%。 
- NP0 主要應用於 AI Server PSU及綠能 AC/DC 轉換，目前供不應求；
Mega Cap 預計 2H26 起大幅放量，帶動 AI 營收占比由上半年 9.2%
提升至 15%～16%以上。

【中信投顧投資分析報告】

個股摘要

202 6 /09 /11

6 
⚫ 下半年漲價幅度及執行狀況？ 
- 大尺寸特殊規格下半年大部分都有漲價，全年平均漲幅約 30%；在
供不應求下 ，部分客戶主動加價搶料 ，並陸續簽訂1 年期 LTA 鎖定
產能。 
⚫ 下半年 AI Server PSU 拉貨動能如何？是否會對主要客戶漲價？ 
- 拉貨自 3Q26 起升溫，4Q26 需求強勁，涵蓋  GB300 及 CSP 專
案；公司不會對核心 AI 客戶大幅漲價，主要透過放量帶動獲利。 
⚫ 公司是否布局 GPU/CPU 計算端？NP0 與 Mega Cap 毛利率如何？ 
- 公司聚焦高壓大尺寸 NP0 及 Mega Cap 等高毛利產品；其中 Mega 
Cap 單價約為一般產品 6～8 倍，有助提升產值及整體毛利率。 
⚫ 擴廠後折舊及費用率對獲利的影響？ 
- 新廠折舊於 2027 年下半年量產後開始提列，營業費用率則維持約
7%。 
⚫ 日系廠商退出高壓大尺寸 X7/X8 市場，台廠為何具優勢？ 
- 日系廠商退出主要係因消費性及低壓小尺寸產能吃緊，並非技術因
素。由於全球約 90% AI Server PSU 集中於台灣，信昌電憑藉在地
化開發、即時服務及快速交貨優勢，成為台灣電源大廠的重要供應
商 。 目前產品交期已拉長至約30 週 ， 公司將優先配置產能予高毛利 
AI 訂單。

電力能源 AIDC 業務成長強勁，預估 3Q26 營收季增 5% 
展望 3Q26，美國資料中心及油氣產業需求維持強勁，東元 2026 年 1-7 月美
國大馬達取單年增近 30% ；東南亞資料中心 AIDC 方面，馬來西亞
Dynaciate 預計 8 月完成交割並開始併表，本中心預估 3Q26 營收季增 5.3%
至 174.8 億元，主要成長來自於電力能源的 AIDC 業務；毛利率估季減 0.8
個 ppts 至 24.4%，季減主因預期較低毛利率的電力能源占比提升；下調稅
後淨利 16.1%至 19.3 億元，EPS 0.82 元，季減 26.9%，主要調整業外收入
金融評價利益與股利收入預估。 
預估 AIDC 業務 2026/2027 年營收占比分別達 8%/12% 
東元 AIDC 短期動能來自 Busway 出貨及 Dynaciate 併表，中期則由 IT、光
纖及 MEP 工程延伸至 MDC，MDC 預計 4Q26 完成 POC，最快 2027 年開
始出貨。東元 2026 年 AIDC 取單目標約 100 億元，前 7 月已取得 50-60 億
元，主要來自 G 客戶馬來西亞及泰國專案，其中 60-70%預計於 2027 年認
列，預估 AIDC 業務 2026/27 年營收占比達 8/12%，金額達 54/93 億元(前
次估 46/147 億)，下修 2027 年營收貢獻主因原預估 2H27 美國資料中心有
望開始挹注營收，然目前客戶於美國遭遇選址問題，尚未看到訂單。  
目前評價合理，維持持有評等 
此次分別上修 2026 年 EPS 6.6% ，主因調整業外收入金融資產評價利益；
下修 2027 年 EPS 5.6% ，主因美國資料中心尚未看到訂單，以及國內離岸
風電以及台電 Statcom 案件遞延，下修電力能源業務預估，預估 2026/2027
年 EPS 分別為 3.15 元/3.52 元。目前評價位於 23 倍/20 倍本益比，為歷史
區間之高檔，目前評價合理，維持持有評等，目標價 80 元，係根據 2027 年
EPS 3.52 元的 23 倍(前次是根據 2026-2027 年平均 EPS 的 23 倍)。 
元大觀點 
⧫  
東元截至 7 月美國大馬達取單年增近 30%；東南亞資料中心方面，馬
來西亞 Dynaciate 預計 8 月完成交割併表，預估 3Q26 營收季增 5%。 
⧫  
東南亞資料中心專案 2026 年取單目標 100 億元；美國市場則尚未看到
實際訂單，預估 AIDC 2026/2027 年營收分別達 54 億/93 億元。 
⧫  
下修 2027 年 EPS 5.6% 至 3.52 元，主因美國資料中心未看到訂單貢
獻，以及國內風電以及台電案件遞延。目前評價合理，維持持有。

營運分析
2Q26 EPS 優於元大/市場預期 47%/42%，主因業外收入高於預期
東元 2Q26 營收季增 16.6%至 166.0 億元，主因「機電系統」亞洲與北美市場維持成長動能，及
「電力能源」之數據中心與離岸風電工程營收貢獻增加；毛利率季增 1.7ppts/年增 1.7ppts 至
25.1%，優於市場預期之 24.6%，主因「電力能源」毛利率提升；營業利益 17.5 億元，季增
48.2%，優於本中心/低於市場預期 7.6%/3.0%；稅後淨利 26.4 億元，季增 121.8%/年增 81.8%，
EPS 1.12 元，優於本中心/市場預期 47.4%/42.2%，主因業外收入認列金融資產評價利益。
四大事業 2Q26 狀況如下：
➢ 機電事業：營收占比 46%，營收年增 13%，主因中國、台灣及北美地區皆呈雙位數成長。
➢ 電力能源：營收占比 26%，營收年增 11%，主因數據中心及離岸風電工程貢獻增加。

➢ 空調科技：營收占比 11%，營收年減 11%，主因產品結構調整所致。
➢ 能源暨系統自動化：營收占比 5%，營收年增 22%，主因自動化產品貢獻增加。

預估東元 AIDC 業務 2026/2027 年營收占比分別達 8%/12%
觀察東元 CSP 客戶東南亞建置規劃，馬來西亞及泰國未來五年 AIDC 新增容量各約 1GW，合計約
2GW，對應年均新增容量約 400MW。若以每 1MW 總建置金額約 10 億元推估，兩地五年整體
AIDC 建置市場規模約 2 兆元、年均約 4,000 億元；其中 MDC 約占建置成本 40%，換算每 1MW
產值約 4 億元，未來五年潛在市場約 8,000 億元。東元 AIDC 短期動能來自 Busway 出貨及

Dynaciate 併表，中期則由 IT、光纖及 MEP 工程延伸至 MDC；MDC 單一模組功率約 2.7MW、交
期約 9 個月，預計 4Q26 完成 POC、最快 2027 年開始出貨。我們認為，東元憑藉東南亞既有工程
實績及少數具備 IT 工程能力的競爭優勢，有望持續提高單 一專案承接範圍與金額。東元 2026 年
AIDC 取單目標約 100 億元，前 7 月已取得 50-60 億元，主要來自馬來西亞及泰國 G 客戶專案，其
中 60-70%預計於 2027 年認列，本中心預估東元 AIDC 業務 2026/2027 年營收占比分別達
8%/12%，金額達 54 億/93 億元(前次估 46 億/147 億，本次下修 2027 年營收貢獻主因原預估
2H27 美國資料中心有望貢獻，然目前因客戶選址問題，尚未看到訂單)。

### Page 4

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：能源設備與服務 2026 年 9 月 8 日 Teco 第4 頁，共 14頁  
產業概況  
模組化資料中心加速 AI 資料中心部署， 2035 年市場規模估將達 1,280 億美元 
全球預製模組化資料中心(Prefabricated Modular Data Centers ，MDC)市場規模預計將由 2025 年
的 293 億美元倍增至 2035 年的 1,280 億美元， CAGR 達 13.8%，主要受惠 AI 驅動算力暴增，單
機櫃功率密度由傳統 5～10 kW 急遽飆升至 100 kW 乃至 1 MW，導致可用故障電流激增，提高電
弧閃絡風險、散熱熱負荷過載，以及液冷系統易燃液體洩漏等重大火災隱患；面對傳統磚造施工週
期冗長與各項安全挑 戰，預製模組化架構將電力、冷卻與  IT 系統於工廠預製整合並通過結構與安
全測試，不僅能大幅加速部署進度、簡化地方主管機關審批流程，更可支援 MW 規模的彈性擴充，
成為解決高功耗安全瓶頸與縮短建置時程的解方。 
圖 4：模組化資料中心市場規模穩健成長，CAGR 達 13.8%

資料來源：UL、元大投顧 
AI 資料中心使美國電網進入升級超級週期 
機構預估 2022-2030 年美國資料中心電力需求 CAGR 達 23%，2022 年資料中心僅佔全美電力需
求 3%，預估 2030 年將成長至 14%。因應 AI 資料中心發展，美國已進入電網升級超級週期，本中
心整理美國電力公用事業資本支出規劃，2025-2030 年約將投入 1.4 兆美金，平均一年約 2,300 億
美元的資本支出，相較過去 6 年約成長 50%，投資項目涵蓋輸配電、發電、再生能源、儲能及工
廠維護等，其中約 60%投資於電網升級與擴增，每年約當創造 1,400 億美金電網工程設備商機。 
圖 5：2022-2030 年美國資料中心電力需求 CAGR 達 23%

資料來源：Mckinsey & Company 2025、元大投顧整理

### Page 5

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：能源設備與服務 2026 年 9 月 8 日 Teco 第5 頁，共 14頁  
強化電網一期專案金額達 2,098 億元，2027-2028 年迎認列高峰 
台電為滿足新竹、臺中、臺南及高雄等科學園區用電成長，提出強化園區負載供電、強化民生負載
供電、強化太陽光電併網及強化南北幹線樞紐等四大強韌相關工程，強化電網第一期專案計畫
2024-2033 年共 10 年預計新建、擴建開閉所 3 所，新建超高壓變電所 4 所、一次配電變電所 5 所
及 345kV 興達～南科線，輸電線路長度 257.5 公里，總預算金額達 2,098 億元。 
觀察目前計畫進度，強韌電網一期至 2025 年專案金額預算投入金額未達 1%，推測主因缺工使台
電土建進度受阻，重電設備拉貨遞延 。展望未來， AI 半導體基礎建設發展快速，經濟部預估
2026~2035 年電力需求年均成長率約為 2.5%，高於過去 10 年年均成長率 1.23%，升級老舊電網、
降低跳電事故，並提供低碳且大量穩定之電力為重要發展關鍵，預估 2027-2028 年強化電網專案
將迎認列高峰，每年投入預算達百億元。 
圖 6：2024-2033 年強化電網一期專案金額達 2,098 億元，2027-2028 年迎認列高峰

### Page 6

獲利調整與股票評價  
目前評價合理，維持持有評等 
此次分別上修 2026 年 EPS 6.6%，主因調整業外收入金融資產評價利益；下修 2027 年 EPS 5.6% ，
主因美國資料中心尚未看到訂單貢獻，以及國內離岸風電以及台電 Statcom 案件遞延，下修電力能
源業務預估，預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 分別為 3.15 元/3.52 元。目前評價位於 23 倍/20 倍本益比，
為歷史區間之高檔，目前評價合理，維持持有評等，目標價 80 元，係根據 2027 年 EPS 3.52 元的
23 倍(前次是根據 2026-2027 年平均 EPS 的 23 倍)。

### Page 10

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：能源設備與服務 2026 年 9 月 8 日 Teco 第10 頁，共 14頁

公司簡介  
東元成立於 1956 年，初期從事馬達生產及行銷，至今已跨入重電、家電、能源等多角化領域，為國內家電領導廠商，大馬
達於美國市佔約 20%、小馬達 5%。東元 2021 年開始積極發展再生能源工程/儲能相關，佔比快速提升至 20%之上，為主
要成長動能。東元產品以北美銷售為主，佔比 40%，北美客戶主要產業為油氣產業；預估公司未來將持續聚焦於 AI 資料中
心/再生能源設備工程、節能/家電設備以及大馬達相關延伸產品三大主軸。

### Page 11

請參閱末頁之免責宣言。 台灣：能源設備與服務 2026 年 9 月 8 日 Teco 第11 頁，共 14頁

ESG 分析  
分別藉由「在 ESG 議題上的曝險」和「個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力」兩個面向來衡量公司於 ESG 上的表現。 
► ESG 總分：東元整體的 ESG 風險評級屬於中風險，於 Sustainalytics 資料庫所覆蓋的公司中排行在領先的位置，亦於
電子設備業的公司中排名領先同業。 
► 在 ESG 議題上的曝險：東元的整體曝險中等，略優於電子設備業的平均水準。公司較需要改善的  ESG 議題包含營運方
面的商業道德、產品治理與人力資本等。 
► 個別公司在 ESG 議題上的執行力：東元在針對重大 ESG 議題的管理水準和執行力強。主因公司的揭露程度極佳，表明
對投資者及公眾的強烈責任感，同時公司於 ESG 亦有相關舉措，但尚不能藉此管理所有風險。儘管如此，東元並未涉
及任何與 ESG 相關的重大爭議。

投資評等說明 
買進：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持正面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中增持該股。 
持有-超越同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力高於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
持有-落後同業：本中心認為根據目前股價，該檔個股基本面吸引力低於 同業。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股發展前景、財務表現、利多題材、評價資訊
以及風險概況之分析。 
賣出：根據本中心對該檔個股投資期間絕對或相對報酬率之預測，我們對該股持負面觀點。此一觀點係基於本中心對該股之發展前景、財務表現、利多題
材、評價資訊以及風險概況之分析。建議投資人於投資部位中減持該股。 
評估中：本中心之預估、評等、目標價尚在評估中，但仍積極追蹤該個股。 
限制評等：為遵循相關法令規章及/或元大之政策，暫不給予評等及目標價。

## Document 453: 凱基投顧_1560 中砂_潘俊宏_20260910

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_1560 中砂_潘俊宏_20260910.pdf

### Page 1

九月 10, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  半導體 ‧ 台灣

中砂 
(1560.TW /1560 TT)

毛利率改善幅度遠優於預期  
重要訊息 
中砂 2Q26 毛利率大幅優於預期，顯示毛利率擴張不僅受鑽石碟（ DBU）銷售
成長帶動，砂輪事業部（ABU）及再生晶圓（SBU）對毛利提升亦有所貢獻。 
評論及分析  
2Q26 毛利率大幅優於預期 。2Q26 營收為 25 億元，季增 8.7%、年增
17.9%，係受惠主要晶圓代工客戶 N2 擴產，其中 N2 相關營收季增 84%；
2Q26 毛利率達 40%，季增 5ppts，優於市場預期 2.7ppts，表現大幅優於預
期，係受各業務毛利率全面改善帶動， ABU、DBU 及 SBU 毛利率在本季分別
提升 6、4、5ppts，過往傳統砂輪通常占 ABU 營收逾 80%，惟在 ABF 載板及
裸晶圓需求強勁帶動下，本季鑽石砂輪營收占比大幅提升至 40%，推升 ABU
毛利率；2Q26 稅後淨利為 5.23億元（EPS 3.49 元），優於市場共識 8%。 
毛利率結構性改善，產能擴張仍有上行空間。中砂將於 3Q26調漲砂輪價格 5-
15%，同時產品組合持續朝再生晶圓及鑽石碟傾斜， 預估整體毛利率將由
2026 年的 40.6%提升至 2027 年的 44.0%，並於 2028 年進一步升至
45.8%，此外，公司預期 DBU 產能將由目前 60kwpm 進一步擴增至 4Q26 的
70kwpm (年增 40%，未計入中國額外 10kwpm 產能），並於 2027 年底擴增
至 95kwpm，SBU產能則將由目前 400kwpm擴增至 2027年底的 430kwpm。 
DBU 主導獲利成長，美系 IDM 客戶帶來潛在上行空間。受惠積極擴產，以及
產品組合改善與漲價帶動 ASP 提升，我們預估 2026 年 DBU 及 SBU 營收將分
別成長 41%及 23%，ABU 業務則相對穩定，但受漲價帶動，我們預估今年營
收可成長 30-40%，中砂在美系 IDM 客戶亦取得正面進展，我們認為有望進一
步強化 DBU 業務，受惠需求強勁成長，我們預估 DBU 營收占比將由 2026 年
的 33%提升至 2027年的 37%，由於 DBU毛利率逾 50%，遠高於公司整體平
均，產品組合持續朝 DBU 傾斜將帶動整體毛利率擴張，而 領先晶圓代工廠積
極擴產仍為中砂主要成長動能，目前我們預估 N2 產能將由現行的 40kwpm 提
升至 2026年底的 80-100kwpm，並於 2027年達 120-130kwpm。 
投資建議  
我們維持「增加持股」評等，基於獲利預估上修及 改以 2028 年每股盈餘作
為評價基準 ，我們將目標價調升至 880 元(仍採 30 倍本益比 )，目前我們
2026 年及 2027 年每股盈餘預估分別較 市場高 12%、15%，受惠領先晶圓
代工廠擴產及零組件在地採購比重提升，我們正面看待中砂營收成長前景 。 
投資風險  
晶圓代工廠產能利用率下滑；先進製程產能爬升較預期緩慢。

中砂成立於 1953 年，總部位於新北市鶯歌區。公司早期業務
以傳統砂輪為主，提供從低階基礎至高階精密「研磨」、「切
削」加工使用的砂輪或刀具等，後期則透過 鑽石碟成功切入半
導體領域。目前中砂以三大事業部為主， 2Q26 砂輪事業部
ABU 加子公司 、鑽石事業部 DBU 及晶圓事業部 SBU 營收比重
分別為 25%、31%與 43%。

## Document 460: 凱基投顧_Takeaway_1477 聚陽_劉昃恩_20260910

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_Takeaway_1477 聚陽_劉昃恩_20260910.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-09-10
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

九月 10, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 1

凱基投資論壇重點摘要

聚陽 
(1477 TT, NT$197.5, 增加持股)

焦點內容 
1. 預計 4Q26 出貨量年增低 個位數，原物料成本上升、產品組合優化及
短交期訂單戰比提升將推升產品 ASP 至年增 5-7%，且毛利率目標達
26%。 
2. 4Q26 短交期訂單佔比預估提升至 15-20%，對比 1Q26 及 3Q26 分別
為 12%、15%。正常短單交期約三週，其中 2-3%超短單交期可縮短
至五日；短單毛利率較一般訂單高約 3 個百分點。  
3. 今年 3 月開始已無需再協助品牌客戶負擔部分關稅成本。去年 6 月至
2026 年 2 月已支付的關稅中，預計 30%將於 2027 年前以現金退回，
其餘則透過產品組合或單價優化等方式返還 。 
結論 
在短交期訂單佔比提升 之加持下，公司 4Q26 指引隱含獲利將延續近 20%
成長，高於市場預期 5-10%。目前凱基給予「增加持股」評等及目標價
253 元。

營運近況更新 
 4Q26 展望：(1)  出貨量年增低個位數；(2) 產品 ASP 年增 5-7%，原物
料成本上升 及產品組合優化分別貢獻 1%、2%，短交期訂單 佔比
15%/20%貢獻約 2%/4%；及 (3) 毛利率目標 26%。  
 預計 4Q26 短交期訂單佔比提升至 15-20%，對比 1Q26 及 3Q26 分別
為 12%、15%。正常短單交期約三 週，其中 2-3%超短單交期可縮 短
至五日；短單毛利率較一般訂單高約 3 個百分點。  
 客戶/產品別方面， 4Q26 百貨公司、品牌商、通路商及運動 盤需求較
佳，Gap  (美) 與日系客戶的 低單價產品表現較弱  (對比日系品牌 1Q-
3Q26 需求強勁)。 
 3Q26 指引不變：(1) 出貨量年增中個位數；(2) 產品 ASP 年減 3%，主
要反映匯率差異；及 (3) 毛利率 24%以上。 
 8 月營收年減，係因去年 美國關稅導致客戶拉貨提前而 墊高基期。今年
出貨恢復正常步調，預計 9月營收維持超過 30 億元水準。 
 預計 2026 年全年出貨量年增個位數，毛利率目標 25%。 
 1Q27 產品組合與 4Q26 相近，4Q26 報價將影響 1Q27 報價區間。公
司 11 月將與客戶討論 2027 年規劃，現階段以穩價為優先。  
 成衣消費景氣並 未明顯改變，但客戶擔憂油價高漲推升 通膨，因此長
單轉趨保守，多以短單替代。現估戰爭影響訂單量 約 3-4%。 
 印尼及孟加拉將持續擴充產能。

凱基投顧

劉昃恩

tseen.liu@kgi.com

重要免責聲明，詳見最終頁

### Page 2

台灣 聚陽

九月 10, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R 2

 受到中東戰爭影響， 約旦及埃及先 以外包方式布局，未來將 視合作情
況評估收購或建廠，長期成長方向不變。  
 印尼 ESG 規範趨嚴，新進者建廠難度提升；公司憑藉多年在地深耕形
成競爭壁壘，具有較高的議價能力。  
 公司工廠多位於二、三線城 市，廠區空間及備用機台充足，具高度生
產彈性。此外，各品牌透過 AI 整合供應鏈，集中採購核心布種並縮減
布種數量，有助降低公司備料複雜度。  
 在電商快速發展下，公司短交期與快速反應能力具備競爭優勢，可配
合縮短交期並降低客戶庫存 。傳統 OEM 若維持 14-16 週交期，毛利率
恐受侵蝕 2-3%。  
 2026 年地區別產能佔比預估：印尼 45% (對比 2025 年為 41%)、越
南 35% (42%) 、柬埔寨 15% (16%) 、中國 2% (2%) 、孟加拉 2% 
(0%)、薩爾瓦多 1% (0%)。   
 今年 3 月開始已無需再協助品牌客戶負擔部分關稅成本。去年 6 月至
2026 年 2 月已支付的關稅中，預計約有 30%將於 2027 年前以現金退
回，其餘則透過產品組合或單價優化等方式返還。  
 1H26 銷貨成本結構： 主料 58%、副料 16%、人工 16%、費用
10%。 
 公司報價已預留約 50-70 個基點的匯率調整空間；若新台幣升值至 29
元，將進一步反應給客戶作 因應。

### Page 1

九月 16, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  1

法說會重點摘要

遠雄 
(5522 TT, NT$60.0, 未評等)

焦點內容 
1. 2H26 共有 7 案陸續完工交屋，包括洄山行、綠美、綠禾、東御
苑、PARK 16、琢蘊、仰森，總銷合計 294 億元，整體營運動能將
顯著優於 1H26。  
2. 大巨蛋出租之百貨已開幕，自營之飯店預計於 4Q26 開幕營運，
2027 年為大巨蛋全項目首次全數營運的年度，公司看好現金流狀
況將顯著改善，目標 2029 年開始對財務產生正向貢獻。 
3. 遠雄目前規劃 2026-29 年預計完工案量依序為 386 億元、309 億
元、220 億元、453 億元。 
結論 
依照遠雄目前建案規畫，未來兩年完工案量將逐年遞減，至 2029年才會
再顯著提升。 目前股價約交易於市場共識 2026 年預計配發現金股利
5.56元之 9.26%殖利率。

營運概況 
 公司現為市值第二大台灣建設開發商，五大競爭優勢涵蓋：規模經
濟、垂直整合 (有效控制成本並維持穩健財務操作 )、低成本土地庫存
(布局台灣全省以分散風險)、以綠能/智慧/性能建築維持高品牌附加價
值，以及具豐富經驗之經營團隊。 
 企業成長策略聚焦三方向：(1)擴展開發區域重心至七大都會區及都市
更新案；(2)投資於固定收益之台北大巨蛋園區與 Outlet 商城；(3)以
品牌價值創造差異化，同時維持穩健財務槍桿。 
 主要轉投資採權益法認列：遠雄巨蛋 (台北大巨蛋 BOT 案，直接持股
25%+透過遠雄營造持股 3%，綜合 28%，帳面投資額 78.00億元)、
三新奧特萊斯 (與日本三井不動產合資之林口 Outlet BOT 案，持股
30%，帳面投資額 7.01億元)。 
 在建建案總銷比例，北部 41%、中部 19%、南部 40%。 
 已售未認列金額接近 800 億元、未售金額 950 億元，成屋剩幸福成
約 100戶(總戶數 5%)、洄山行約 20億元。 
 2027 年預計推案：泰山廠辦案 3Q27（總銷 50~60 億元）、桃園兩
案 1Q27（總銷合計約 300 億元）、台南住宅案（總銷 60 億元）、
高雄住宅案（總銷約 260~280 億元），以上五案合計總銷 720 億
元。 
 現金股利配發率大概維持 70%。

大巨蛋狀況 
 遠東百貨於近期開幕，飯店預計 4Q26開幕營運。 
 全部開幕後，預計 2027 年折舊金額會高於今年，損益表會看比較保
守，但現金流會有顯著助益。 
 公司目標 2029年開始產生效益，但也要看營運狀況。

市況及其他 
 預售狀況，今年雙北預售都比預期好 (北士科明月、和雅 )。台南、高
雄預期本來就比較保守，推案銷售狀況也符合預期。 
 預售促銷方式以低自備付款配套為主，不會採用送裝修的方式。 
 今年房市銷售狀況，雙北 >中南部，主要是房市貸款緊縮的影響，除
了銀行資金優先放款給電子產業外，民眾有資金也會傾向買股優先於
買房。 
 考量房市保守，公司今年設定的潛銷期銷售目標 15~20%，照這樣的
速度推進，使照前應該可以完銷。 
 公司預期選舉過後，2027年房市狀況應該會比今年好。

投資評等  買進

全新(2455) 
目標價位  600 元

明年又是全新的一年

公司治理評鑑排序： 
1 2 3 4 5 6 7

個股基本資料：

江秉恩 
網通、光通訊 
S1138003@tss-c.com.tw 
 股價評價基礎： 
全新 2Q26 營收 9.65 億元，QoQ+0.6%，稅後淨利 2.14 億元，EPS 1.15
元，營運符合預期。展望 3Q26，全新營運可望延續成長動能，主要受惠於 
AI 與高速光通訊需求持續擴張。在產業面上，資料中心升級帶動 800G 以
上光模組滲透率提升 ， 推升上游磊晶與雷射元件需求 ；全新在 InP 磊晶與光
電元件具備技術優勢，將直接受惠於光通訊與高速傳輸應用放量，全年營收
可望雙位數成長，營運進入新一輪上行循環。投顧預估全新 3Q26 營收 13.6
億元 ，QoQ+40.7%，YoY+48.8%，EPS 2.06 元。在毛利率部分 ， 全新2Q26
已進行報價調整，光電子產品基板供應狀況伴隨補件審批通過，順利分批取
得部分料源，未來在上游廠商持續積極擴產下，基板瓶頸有望逐步緩解，故
預估下半年毛利率將明顯優於上半年。整體而言，2026 年全新微電子業務
透過 WiFi7 相關產品支撐營運基礎，而光電子業務自 2Q26 起小規模量產
200G PD 及 400G GaAs 新品，且發射端產品客戶有望增加，應用領域包含
EML 及 CW Laser，2027 年發射端展望能見度有所提升。資本支出部分，全
新積極投入購置 MOCVD 與 Ebeam 等機台設備擴充產能，MOCVD 新機台
預計今年陸續進場整機，因應光電子業務明年之訂單需求。 投顧預估全新
2026/2027 年營收分別將達 46.9/64.9 億元，+38.6%/38.4% YoY，EPS 為 
6.52/11.91 元 。 考量公司2027 年光電子業務成長趨勢明確且基板供應問題
逐步緩解，我們給予近年評價區間上緣約 50 倍本益比，故給予買進投資評
等，目標價 600 元(50.4x 2027 EPS)，潛在漲幅 16.5%(以 2026/9/16 收盤
價 515 元計算)。

營運近況摘要： 
全新為 全球重要 MOCVD 磊晶製造商  
全新光電科技股份有限公司成立於 1996 年 11 月 26 日，總部位於桃園市平
鎮區， 主要以MOCVD 技術生產砷化鎵(GaAs)、磷化銦(InP)磊晶 ，為全球前
三大製造商之一，2002 年 1 月 24 日上市。公司以 MOCVD 為核心技術生
產三五族化合物半導體磊晶片，主要產品為微波通訊產品及光電子產品，三
五族化合物半導體具備高頻低雜訊、抗天然輻射及能源使用率佳等優勢，為
無線通訊 、 光纖通訊 、 光學感測 、 太陽能電池及光顯示之關鍵組件。2Q26 產
品營收結構為微電子 72.9%、光電子 27.1%。

個股報告 September 17, 2026

### Page 2

2 
全新八月營收續創新高， 基板供應問題緩解 有助毛利率 提升 
全新 2Q26 營收 9.65 億元，QoQ+0.6%，稅後淨利 2.14 億元，EPS 1.15
元 ， 營運符合預期 。 而全新 8 月營收達 4.71 億元，+11.56% MoM，+71.16% 
YoY，寫下歷史單月新高。展望 2H26，光電子產品基板供應狀況伴隨補件審
批通過，順利分批取得部分料源，相較於 2Q26 光電子營收占比僅 27.1%，
全新七、八月光電子營收占比已逾四成，我們認為在基板禁令放寬且未來上
游廠商持續積極擴產的趨勢下，2H26 基板供應問題將有效緩解，明年相關
原物料供應亦轉趨正向，故預估下半年毛利率將明顯優於上半年。因此我們
同步上修 3Q26 營運展望 ，投顧預估全新 3Q26 營收為 13.58 億元 ，+40.7% 
QoQ，+48.8% YoY，毛利率 46.4%，EPS 為 2.06 元。

2027 年接收端高階產品放量， 發射端客戶 能見度提升  
微電子業務上，手機相關應用雖面臨產業逆風，但整體仍透過WiFi7 相關產
品支撐營運基礎，而光電子業務自 2Q26 起小規模量產 200G PD 及 400G 
GaAs 產品，2027 年接收端高階產品有望進一步放量，此外，我們認為全新
在發射端客戶能見度有所提升，持續有客戶於全新投入研發單，未來有望於
全新量產，產品涵蓋 EML 及 CW Laser 等應用。因此我們同步上修今明年公
司營運展望，投顧預估 2027 年全新光電子業務全年營收占比將逾五成，帶
動營收規模與毛利率同步成長。

全新持續投入資本支出購置機台及擴廠，進度符合預期 
資本支出部分 ， 全新積極投入購置MOCVD 與 Ebeam 等機台設備擴充產能 ，
MOCVD 新機台預計今年陸續進廠裝機，因應光電子業務明年之訂單需求。
其中 4 台 MOCVD 新設備將於 3Q26 陸續進廠，4Q26 準備量產；Ebeam
新機台則將於 1Q27 進廠，預估全新擴產後 2027 年將擁有 64 台 MOCVD
及 3 台 Ebeam 機台設備，目前公司持續尋找新廠地點，我們認為擴廠進度
亦符合預期，有助全新支應 2027 年主要客戶潛在訂單需求。

投顧預估全新 2026 年營收為 46.9 億元，+38.6% YoY，主要受惠於 AI 與
高速光通訊需求持續擴張，伴隨基板問題逐步緩解，光電子產品放量速度提
升，預估全年毛利率為 43.7%，YoY +7.6 ppt。預估營業利益率為 30.5%，
歸屬母公司稅後淨利為 12.1 億元，EPS 為 6.52 元。

投資建議 
合庫預估2026F/2027F稅後EPS 5.23/4.63元，
由於記憶體價格 維持高檔， IDC預估2026、
2027 年 全 球 手 機 出 貨 量 分 別 年 減16.7% 、
2.9%，出貨量持續衰退對宏捷科營運產生不
利影響，雖新業務陸續於2027年貢獻營收，
仍無法抵銷手機需求衰退影響，故投資建議
下調至中立。 
營收比重

公司簡介  
宏捷科(8086)為國內砷化鎵晶圓代工廠，主要
競爭對手為穩懋(3105)， 主要生產功率放大器 、
射頻元件及VCSEL。 客戶包含紫光展銳、唯捷創
芯 (688153.CN) 、 飛驤科技 、 康希通信 及
Qualcomm(QCOM.US)等。2Q26營收按照終端
應 用 分 別 為Wi-Fi 20~25% 、手機 70~75% 及
VCSEL 5~6%。
